``````text
const HERE = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const SOURCE = path.resolve(HERE, '../assets/directed-motion');
const DEFAULT_CONFIG = 'E:/开发项目/视频剪辑skill/config/video-workflow.json';
const DEFAULT_FONT = 'C:/Windows/Fonts/msyh.ttc';
const KINDS = ['orb','platform','gate','house','person','line','text','asset'];
const PROPS = ['x','y','rotation','scale','scaleX','scaleY','opacity','light','progress'];
function check(ok, m) { if (!ok) throw new Error(m); }
function object(v,l) { check(v && typeof v==='object' && !Array.isArray(v),`${l} must be an object`);return v; }
function keys(v,allowed,l) { for(const k of Object.keys(v))check(allowed.includes(k),`${l}: unknown field ${k}`); }
function number(v,l,min,max) { check(typeof v==='number'&&Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=min&&v<=max,`${l} must be ${min}..${max}`);return v; }
function text(v,l,max=120,visible=true) { check(typeof v==='string' && v.length>0 && v.length<=max && !/[\x00-\x08\x0b\x0c\x0e-\x1f]/.test(v),`${l} needs plain text up to ${max} characters`);if(visible)assertVisibleText(v,l);return v.normalize('NFC'); }
function color(v,l) { check(/^#[\da-f]{6}$/i.test(v),`${l} must be #RRGGBB`);return v; }
function id(v,l) { check(typeof v==='string'&&/^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,70}$/.test(v),`${l} needs ASCII identifier`);return v; }
function args(argv) {
  const o={};for(let i=0;i<argv.length;i++) { const k=argv[i];if(k==='--help')return{help:true};check(['--input','--output','--config','--gsap','--font'].includes(k),`Unknown flag ${k}`);check(!o[k.slice(2)],`Duplicate ${k}`);check(argv[i+1]&&!argv[i+1].startsWith('--'),`Missing ${k}`);o[k.slice(2)]=argv[++i]; }
  check(o.input&&o.output,'--input and --output are required');return o;
}
async function exists(p) { try{await fs.lstat(p);return true;}catch(e){if(e.code==='ENOENT')return false;throw e;} }
const html = v=>String(v).replace(/[&<>"']/g,x=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'})[x]);
const json = v=>JSON.stringify(v).replace(/[<>&\u2028\u2029]/g,c=>`\\u${c.charCodeAt(0).toString(16).padStart(4,'0')}`);
const hash = b=>createHash('sha256').update(b).digest('hex');

// Preview UI is a separate document. Its wall-clock drives only seeks into the
// exported, paused deterministic composition and never enters index.html.
function previewPage(spec) {
  return `<!doctype html><html lang="zh-CN"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><title>${html(spec.title)} · 源码预览</title><style>html,body{margin:0;background:#111923;color:#e7eef5;font-family:sans-serif}main{max-width:1000px;margin:0 auto;padding:20px}h1{font-size:22px;margin:0 0 14px}.viewer{position:relative;width:100%;aspect-ratio:${spec.canvas.width}/${spec.canvas.height};overflow:hidden;background:#000}iframe{position:absolute;left:0;top:0;width:${spec.canvas.width}px;height:${spec.canvas.height}px;border:0;transform-origin:0 0}.controls{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;padding:14px 0;flex-wrap:wrap}button{border:1px solid #536779;border-radius:6px;padding:9px 14px;background:#26394a;color:#e7eef5;cursor:pointer}button:disabled{opacity:.4;cursor:wait}input{flex:1;min-width:180px}output{font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;font-size:14px;white-space:nowrap}.note{font-size:14px;line-height:1.6;color:#a7bbc9}</style></head><body><main><h1>${html(spec.title)}</h1><div class="viewer" id="viewer"><iframe id="film-frame" src="index.html" title="${html(spec.title)}"></iframe></div><div class="controls"><button id="play-toggle" type="button" disabled>播放</button><button id="replay" type="button" disabled>重播</button><input id="scrubber" type="range" min="0" max="${spec.canvas.duration}" step="${1/spec.canvas.fps}" value="0" aria-label="时间" disabled><output id="position">0.00 / ${spec.canvas.duration.toFixed(2)} 秒</output></div><p class="note" id="status">正在加载本地工程…</p><p class="note">这些控件只用于源码预览，视频输出不包含它们。成片审查优先直接播放交付 MP4。</p></main><script>
  const frame=document.getElementById('film-frame'),viewer=document.getElementById('viewer'),playButton=document.getElementById('play-toggle'),replayButton=document.getElementById('replay'),slider=document.getElementById('scrubber'),position=document.getElementById('position'),status=document.getElementById('status');
  const duration=${spec.canvas.duration},fps=${spec.canvas.fps},compositionId=${json(spec.compositionId)};
  let adapter=null,currentTime=0,playing=false,raf=0,clockStart=0,playStart=0;
  function fit(){frame.style.transform='scale('+(viewer.clientWidth/${spec.canvas.width})+')';}
  new ResizeObserver(fit).observe(viewer);fit();
  function seek(value){currentTime=Math.max(0,Math.min(duration,Number(value)||0));if(adapter)adapter.seek(currentTime);slider.value=String(currentTime);position.textContent=currentTime.toFixed(2)+' / '+duration.toFixed(2)+' 秒';}
  function pause(){playing=false;cancelAnimationFrame(raf);raf=0;playButton.textContent='播放';}
  function tick(now){if(!playing)return;const t=Math.min(duration,playStart+(now-clockStart)/1000);seek(Math.floor(t*fps+1e-7)/fps);if(t>=duration){seek(duration);pause();return;}raf=requestAnimationFrame(tick);}
  function play(){if(!adapter)return;if(currentTime>=duration)seek(0);playing=true;clockStart=performance.now();playStart=currentTime;playButton.textContent='暂停';raf=requestAnimationFrame(tick);}
  function replay(){pause();seek(0);play();}
  playButton.addEventListener('click',()=>playing?pause():play());replayButton.addEventListener('click',replay);slider.addEventListener('input',()=>{pause();seek(slider.value);});
  async function attach(attempt=0){try{const child=frame.contentWindow,directed=child.__directed,timeline=child.__timelines&&child.__timelines[compositionId];if(!directed&&!timeline){if(attempt<180){requestAnimationFrame(()=>attach(attempt+1));return;}throw new Error('工程未完成初始化');}await child.document.fonts.ready;adapter={seek(t){if(directed)directed.seek(t);else{timeline.pause();timeline.totalTime(t,false);}}};seek(0);playButton.disabled=false;replayButton.disabled=false;slider.disabled=false;status.textContent='已加载：本地 HTTP 源码预览';}catch(error){status.textContent='加载失败，请使用本地 HTTP 服务打开 preview.html。'+error.message;}}
  frame.addEventListener('load',()=>attach());
  window.__preview={seek(t){pause();seek(t);},play,pause,replay,state(){return{ready:Boolean(adapter),time:currentTime,playing,duration,fps};}};
  if(location.protocol==='file:')status.textContent='请先启动本地 HTTP 服务，不能直接以 file:// 打开。';
  </script></body></html>`;
}

async function validate(raw,input,gsap,font) {
  object(raw,'input');keys(raw,['schemaVersion','compositionId','title','canvas','style','camera','nodes','tracks','captions','backgroundAsset'],'input');
  check(raw.schemaVersion===1,'schemaVersion must be 1');
  const compositionId=id(raw.compositionId??'directed-motion','compositionId');
  const title=text(raw.title??'生活的缓冲','title',120,false);
  const c=object(raw.canvas,'canvas');keys(c,['width','height','fps','duration'],'canvas');
  for(const key of ['width','height','fps','duration'])check(Object.hasOwn(c,key),`canvas.${key} is required; inherit the actual project specification explicitly`);
  const canvas={width:number(c.width,'canvas.width',320,4096),height:number(c.height,'canvas.height',240,4096),fps:number(c.fps,'canvas.fps',1,120),duration:number(c.duration,'canvas.duration',.5,600)};
  check(Number.isInteger(canvas.width)&&Number.isInteger(canvas.height)&&Number.isInteger(canvas.fps),'canvas width/height/fps must be integers');
  const style={background:'#07111C',foreground:'#EDF3F5',accent:'#F1BD71',lightX:-.6,lightY:-.8,...object(raw.style??{},'style')};keys(style,['background','foreground','accent','lightX','lightY'],'style');
  for(const k of ['background','foreground','accent'])color(style[k],`style.${k}`);for(const k of ['lightX','lightY'])number(style[k],`style.${k}`,-1,1);
  check(Math.hypot(style.lightX,style.lightY)>.1,'Main light direction cannot be zero');
  const camera={x:canvas.width/2,y:canvas.height/2,zoom:1,...object(raw.camera??{},'camera')};keys(camera,['x','y','zoom'],'camera');number(camera.x,'camera.x',-50000,50000);number(camera.y,'camera.y',-50000,50000);number(camera.zoom,'camera.zoom',.1,5);
  const assets=[];
  async function asset(value,kind) {
    check(typeof value==='string'&&value&&!value.includes('\0'),`${kind}: needs local file`);check(!/^[a-z][a-z\d+.-]*:/i.test(value)||/^[a-z]:[\\/]/i.test(value),`${kind}: URL not allowed`);
    const p=path.resolve(path.dirname(input),value),stat=await fs.stat(p);check(stat.isFile()&&stat.size>0,`${kind}: not a nonempty file`);
    const buffer=await fs.readFile(p),ext=path.extname(p).toLowerCase();
    if(kind==='image') {
      check(['.png','.jpg','.jpeg','.webp','.svg'].includes(ext),'Images must be SVG, PNG, JPEG or WebP');
      if(ext==='.svg'){
        const s=buffer.toString('utf8').replace(/<!--[\s\S]*?-->/g,'');
        check(/<svg\b/i.test(s),'SVG root missing');
        check(!/<(?:script|foreignObject|animate|animateTransform|set)\b|\bon\w+\s*=|(?:javascript:|data:|@import)|(?:href|src)\s*=\s*["'][^#"']|url\(\s*["']?(?!#)/i.test(s),'SVG cannot include active content, timed animation or external resources');
        for(const match of s.matchAll(/<(?:text|tspan|textPath)\b[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/(?:text|tspan|textPath)>/gi)){
          const visible=match[1].replace(/<[^>]*>/g,'').replace(/&#x([\da-f]+);/gi,(_,hex)=>String.fromCodePoint(parseInt(hex,16))).replace(/&#(\d+);/g,(_,num)=>String.fromCodePoint(Number(num))).replace(/&amp;/g,'&').replace(/&lt;/g,'<').replace(/&gt;/g,'>').replace(/&quot;/g,'"').replace(/&apos;/g,"'");assertVisibleText(visible,'SVG visible text');
        }
      }else{
        check((ext==='.png'&&buffer.subarray(0,8).equals(Buffer.from([137,80,78,71,13,10,26,10])))||(['.jpg','.jpeg'].includes(ext)&&buffer[0]===255&&buffer[1]===216&&buffer[2]===255)||(ext==='.webp'&&buffer.toString('ascii',0,4)==='RIFF'&&buffer.toString('ascii',8,12)==='WEBP'),'Raster image signature does not match extension');
      }
    }
    if(kind==='gsap')check(ext==='.js'&&/GSAP\s+\d+\.\d+\.\d+/i.test(buffer.toString('utf8')),'GSAP file banner missing');
    if(kind==='font')check(['.ttc','.ttf','.otf','.woff','.woff2'].includes(ext),'Unsupported font format');
    const digest=hash(buffer),relativePath=kind==='gsap'?'assets/gsap.min.js':`assets/${digest}${ext}`;
    const existing=assets.find(a=>a.sha256===digest);if(existing)return existing.relativePath;
    assets.push({kind,originalPath:p,relativePath,sha256:digest,bytes:stat.size,buffer});return relativePath;
  }
  await asset(gsap,'gsap');const fontAsset=await asset(font,'font');
  let backgroundAsset=null;if(raw.backgroundAsset)backgroundAsset=await asset(raw.backgroundAsset,'image');
  check(Array.isArray(raw.nodes)&&raw.nodes.length>0&&raw.nodes.length<=150,'nodes need 1..150 objects');const ids=new Set();
  const nodes=[];
  for(const [i,rawNode]of raw.nodes.entries()) {
    const l=`nodes[${i}]`,n=object(rawNode,l);keys(n,['id','kind','x','y','depth','width','height','radius','rotation','scale','scaleX','scaleY','opacity','light','progress','groundY','shadow','text','size','style','assetPath'],l);

    check(KINDS.includes(node.kind),`${l}.kind unsupported`);check(!ids.has(node.id),`${l}.id duplicates another node: ${node.id}`);check(node.id!=='camera',`${l}.id camera is reserved for the camera track target`);ids.add(node.id);check(typeof node.shadow==='boolean',`${l}.shadow must be boolean`);

async function main(){const a=args(process.argv.slice(2));if(a.help){console.log('build-directed-motion.mjs --input local-scene.json --output NEW_DIRECTORY [--config WORKSPACE_CONFIG] [--gsap LOCAL_JS] [--font LOCAL_FONT]');return;}
  const input=path.resolve(a.input),output=path.resolve(a.output);check(!await exists(output),`Output already exists: ${output}`);

Use --config or explicit --gsap and --font.`);}}

  try{

    await fs.copyFile(path.join(SOURCE,'evaluator.mjs'),path.join(staging,'assets/evaluator.mjs'));
    await fs.copyFile(path.join(SOURCE,'player.mjs'),path.join(staging,'assets/player.mjs'));

    await fs.writeFile(path.join(staging,'scene.json'),JSON.stringify(spec,null,2)+'\n',{flag:'wx'});
    await fs.writeFile(path.join(staging,'input-original.json'),JSON.stringify(raw,null,2)+'\n',{flag:'wx'});
    const manifest={kind:'directed-motion-asset-manifest',compositionId:spec.compositionId,canvas:spec.canvas,offline:true,audio:'none',renderer:'hyperframes',assets:assets.map(({buffer,...rest})=>rest),capabilities:['independent-local-time-tracks','persistent-object-identities','2d-depth-camera-projection','deterministic-gravity-spring-hermite','local-light-and-shadow'],limitations:['no-3d-physics','no-automatic-directing','no-audio','no-core-state-mutation']};
    await fs.writeFile(path.join(staging,'asset-manifest.json'),JSON.stringify(manifest,null,2)+'\n',{flag:'wx'});
    await fs.writeFile(path.join(staging,'README.md'),`# ${spec.title}\n\n离线动态图形工程，${spec.canvas.width}×${spec.canvas.height}，${spec.canvas.fps}fps，${spec.canvas.duration}秒。音轨：无。成片审查优先直接播放交付 MP4。运行资源均在本工程内。状态说明和素材来源留在交付文档，不绘制在视频。\n\n## 源码预览\n\n不能双击以 file:// 打开 HTML，浏览器会阻止本工程的 ES 模块。请用已安装的 Python 3.12 内置本地 HTTP 服务提供本工程，再打开独立的 preview.html；它在 iframe 外提供播放、暂停、重播和时间滑块，控件不会写入 index.html 或视频帧。\n\n\`\`\`powershell\n& "C:/Users/MSN/AppData/Local/Programs/Python/Python312/python.exe" -m http.server 3108 --bind 127.0.0.1 --directory "${output.split(path.sep).join('/')}"\n\`\`\`\n\n在浏览器打开 http://127.0.0.1:3108/preview.html 。命令在当前终端前台运行，结束时 Ctrl+C。工作区外依照实际本地工具选择已验证的 HTTP 服务，不假定有上述 Python 路径。HyperFrames Studio 在本机仅证实服务可启动，UI 加载未通过验收，不作为本交付的可用预览入口。构建器不会安装、后台启动或发布服务。\n\npreview.html 的浏览器时钟只负责预览进度，按帧定位暂停的确定性工程；index.html、evaluator 和导出时间轴不读取它。运行时窗口 __directed.seek(t) 可定位，最终渲染使用有限暂停 GSAP 时间轴。字幕、图形与运动来自 input-original.json。2D光影与分层投影是艺术近似，不是真实物理解算。工程不管理既有核心项目状态；事实与创意核对、浏览器动作前中后与反向定位检查、最终MP4解码和观看应分别记录。技术检查通过不代表用户成片确认。\n`,{flag:'wx'});
    // Rename is exclusive at the caller level; a competing output creator is checked again.

  console.log(JSON.stringify({ok:true,output,index:path.join(output,'index.html'),duration:spec.canvas.duration,nodes:spec.nodes.length,tracks:spec.tracks.length,audio:'none',networkUsed:false},null,2));
}
main().catch(e=>{console.error(e.message);process.exitCode=1;});
``````

``````text
#!/usr/bin/env node
/** Build a new, offline HyperFrames motion composition. No core project state. */
import * as fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import { createReadStream } from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { createHash, randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { assertVisibleText } from './visible-text-policy.mjs';

const HERE = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const COMPONENTS = path.resolve(HERE, '../assets/motion-components');
const RECIPES = new Set(['title-reveal', 'photo-pan', 'flow-steps', 'comparison', 'checklist', 'metric-bars', 'quote-focus', 'lower-third']);
const EPS = 1e-7;
function check(ok, message) { if (!ok) throw new Error(message); }
function object(value, label) { check(value && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value), `${label} must be an object`); return value; }
function keys(value, allowed, label) { for (const key of Object.keys(value)) check(allowed.includes(key), `${label}: unknown field ${key}`); }
function number(value, label, min, max) { check(typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value) && value >= min && value <= max, `${label} must be finite and between ${min} and ${max}`); return value; }
function string(value, label, max = 120, required = true) {
  if (value === undefined && !required) return '';
  check(typeof value === 'string' && !/[\x00-\x08\x0b\x0c\x0e-\x1f]/.test(value), `${label} must be plain text`);
  const result = value.normalize('NFC').trim();
  check(!required || result.length > 0, `${label} must not be empty`);
  check(Array.from(result).length <= max, `${label} exceeds ${max} characters`);
  return result;
}
function visibleString(value, label, max = 120, required = true) {
  return assertVisibleText(string(value, label, max, required), label);
}
function array(value, label, min, max) { check(Array.isArray(value) && value.length >= min && value.length <= max, `${label} needs ${min}..${max} items`); return value; }
function html(value) { return String(value).replace(/[&<>"']/g, x => ({ '&':'&amp;', '<':'&lt;', '>':'&gt;', '"':'&quot;', "'":'&#39;' })[x]); }
function jsonForHtml(value) { return JSON.stringify(value).replace(/[<>&\u2028\u2029]/g, x => `\\u${x.charCodeAt(0).toString(16).padStart(4, '0')}`); }
async function exists(filename) { try { await fs.lstat(filename); return true; } catch (e) { if (e.code === 'ENOENT') return false; throw e; } }
async function sha256(filename) { const hash = createHash('sha256'); for await (const part of createReadStream(filename)) hash.update(part); return hash.digest('hex'); }
function localPath(value, base, label) {
  check(typeof value === 'string' && value.trim() && !value.includes('\0'), `${label} needs a local path`);
  check(!/^[a-z][a-z\d+.-]*:/i.test(value) || /^[a-z]:[\\/]/i.test(value), `${label} cannot be a URL`);
  return path.resolve(base, value);
}
async function localFile(filename, label) { const stat = await fs.stat(filename).catch(e => { throw new Error(`${label}: unreadable file ${filename} (${e.code})`); }); check(stat.isFile() && stat.size > 0, `${label} must be a nonempty file`); return stat; }

function cli(argv) {
  const result = {};
  const names = new Set(['--input', '--output', '--gsap', '--font']);
  for (let i = 0; i < argv.length; i++) {
    const key = argv[i];
    if (key === '--help') { check(argv.length === 1, '--help cannot be combined with other flags'); return { help: true }; }
    check(names.has(key), `Unknown flag: ${key}`);
    check(!Object.hasOwn(result, key.slice(2)), `Duplicate flag: ${key}`);
    check(typeof argv[i + 1] === 'string' && !argv[i + 1].startsWith('--'), `Missing value for ${key}`);
    result[key.slice(2)] = argv[++i];
  }
  for (const key of ['input', 'output', 'gsap']) check(result[key], `--${key} is required`);
  return result;
}

async function validate(raw, inputPath, gsapPath, fontPath) {
  object(raw, 'input'); keys(raw, ['schemaVersion', 'compositionId', 'title', 'canvas', 'style', 'shots', 'captions'], 'input');
  check(raw.schemaVersion === 1, 'schemaVersion must be 1');
  const compositionId = string(raw.compositionId, 'compositionId', 80);
  check(/^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_-]*$/.test(compositionId), 'compositionId must be an ASCII identifier');
  const canvas = object(raw.canvas, 'canvas'); keys(canvas, ['width', 'height', 'fps', 'duration', 'transparent', 'captionReserve'], 'canvas');
  const width = number(canvas.width, 'canvas.width', 320, 8192), height = number(canvas.height, 'canvas.height', 240, 8192);
  check(Number.isInteger(width) && Number.isInteger(height), 'canvas width and height must be integers');
  const fps = number(canvas.fps ?? 30, 'canvas.fps', 1, 120), duration = number(canvas.duration, 'canvas.duration', .5, 3600);
  const transparent = canvas.transparent ?? false; check(typeof transparent === 'boolean', 'canvas.transparent must be boolean');
  const captionReserve = number(canvas.captionReserve ?? .14, 'canvas.captionReserve', .1, .3);
  const style = { background: '#F4EFE5', foreground: '#173B36', muted: '#526963', accent: '#D05A32', ...object(raw.style ?? {}, 'style') };
  keys(style, ['background', 'foreground', 'muted', 'accent'], 'style');
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(style)) check(/^#[\da-f]{6}$/i.test(value), `style.${key} must be #RRGGBB`);
  const title = string(raw.title, 'title', 120, false) || '可复用动效';
  check(compositionId !== 'motion-description', 'compositionId conflicts with the internal description element');
  const ids = new Set([compositionId, 'motion-description']);
  const assets = new Map();
  async function asset(filename, kind, use, outputName) {
    const stat = await localFile(filename, kind);
    if (!assets.has(filename)) {
      const hash = await sha256(filename), ext = path.extname(filename).toLowerCase();
      assets.set(filename, { originalPath: filename, realPath: await fs.realpath(filename), sha256: hash, bytes: stat.size, kind, relativePath: outputName || `assets/${hash}${ext}`, uses: [] });
    }
    const entry = assets.get(filename); check(entry.kind === kind, `Asset used as conflicting kinds: ${filename}`); entry.uses.push(use); return entry;
  }
  check(path.extname(gsapPath).toLowerCase() === '.js', '--gsap must point to a local JavaScript file');
  await asset(gsapPath, 'gsap-runtime', 'animation-engine', 'assets/gsap.min.js');
  const gsapText = await fs.readFile(gsapPath, 'utf8');
  const gsapVersion = /GSAP\s+(\d+\.\d+\.\d+)/i.exec(gsapText)?.[1] ?? null;
  check(gsapVersion, '--gsap file does not contain a recognizable GSAP version banner');
  let font = null;
  if (fontPath) {
    const ext = path.extname(fontPath).toLowerCase(); check(['.ttf', '.otf', '.ttc', '.woff', '.woff2'].includes(ext), 'Supported font extensions: TTF, OTF, TTC, WOFF, WOFF2');
    font = await asset(fontPath, 'font', 'local-typeface');
  }
  function timed(item, label) {
    const id = string(item.id, `${label}.id`, 80); check(/^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_-]*$/.test(id), `${label}.id must be an ASCII identifier`); check(!ids.has(id), `Duplicate id: ${id}`); ids.add(id);
    const start = number(item.start, `${label}.start`, 0, duration), itemDuration = number(item.duration, `${label}.duration`, .5, duration);
    check(start + itemDuration <= duration + EPS, `${label} exceeds canvas.duration`);
    return { id, start, duration: itemDuration };
  }
  const shots = [];
  for (const [i, shot] of array(raw.shots, 'shots', 1, 200).entries()) {
    const label = `shots[${i}]`; object(shot, label); keys(shot, ['id', 'start', 'duration', 'recipe', 'params', 'source'], label);
    const normal = timed(shot, label); check(RECIPES.has(shot.recipe), `${label}.recipe is unsupported`); normal.recipe = shot.recipe;
    check(!transparent || normal.recipe === 'lower-third', 'Transparent canvas only supports lower-third overlays');
    const p = object(shot.params, `${label}.params`), pl = `${label}.params`;
    normal.source = visibleString(shot.source, `${label}.source`, 220, ['comparison', 'metric-bars'].includes(normal.recipe));
    const optional = (key, max) => visibleString(p[key], `${pl}.${key}`, max, false);
    switch (normal.recipe) {
      case 'title-reveal': keys(p, ['eyebrow', 'title', 'subtitle'], pl); normal.params = { eyebrow: optional('eyebrow', 32), title: visibleString(p.title, `${pl}.title`, 52), subtitle: optional('subtitle', 100) }; break;
      case 'photo-pan': {
        keys(p, ['image', 'title', 'subtitle', 'pan', 'positionX', 'positionY', 'zoomStart', 'zoomEnd'], pl);
        const filename = localPath(p.image, path.dirname(inputPath), `${pl}.image`), ext = path.extname(filename).toLowerCase();
        check(['.png', '.jpg', '.jpeg', '.webp'].includes(ext), `${pl}.image must be PNG, JPEG or WebP`);
        const item = await asset(filename, 'image', normal.id);
        const handle = await fs.open(filename, 'r'); const magic = Buffer.alloc(12); await handle.read(magic, 0, 12, 0); await handle.close();
        check((ext === '.png' && magic.subarray(0, 8).equals(Buffer.from([137,80,78,71,13,10,26,10]))) || (['.jpg','.jpeg'].includes(ext) && magic[0] === 255 && magic[1] === 216 && magic[2] === 255) || (ext === '.webp' && magic.toString('ascii', 0, 4) === 'RIFF' && magic.toString('ascii', 8, 12) === 'WEBP'), `${pl}.image signature does not match extension`);
        const pan = p.pan ?? 'left'; check(['left','right','up','down','none'].includes(pan), `${pl}.pan must be left/right/up/down/none`);
        normal.params = { image: item.relativePath, title: visibleString(p.title, `${pl}.title`, 48), subtitle: optional('subtitle', 80), pan, positionX: number(p.positionX ?? 50, `${pl}.positionX`, 0, 100), positionY: number(p.positionY ?? 50, `${pl}.positionY`, 0, 100), zoomStart: number(p.zoomStart ?? 1.12, `${pl}.zoomStart`, 1.04, 1.4), zoomEnd: number(p.zoomEnd ?? 1.05, `${pl}.zoomEnd`, 1.04, 1.4) }; break;
      }
      case 'flow-steps': case 'checklist': {
        keys(p, ['title', 'items', 'note'], pl);
        normal.params = { title: visibleString(p.title, `${pl}.title`, 48), note: optional('note', 100), items: array(p.items, `${pl}.items`, 2, 5).map((item, index) => { const il = `${pl}.items[${index}]`; object(item, il); keys(item, ['label','detail'], il); return { label: visibleString(item.label, `${il}.label`, 24), detail: visibleString(item.detail, `${il}.detail`, 58, false) }; }) }; break;
      }
      case 'comparison': {
        keys(p, ['title', 'left', 'right', 'rows', 'note'], pl);
        normal.params = { title: visibleString(p.title, `${pl}.title`, 48), left: visibleString(p.left, `${pl}.left`, 20), right: visibleString(p.right, `${pl}.right`, 20), note: optional('note', 80), rows: array(p.rows, `${pl}.rows`, 1, 4).map((row, index) => { const rl = `${pl}.rows[${index}]`; object(row, rl); keys(row, ['label','left','right'], rl); return { label: visibleString(row.label, `${rl}.label`, 20), left: visibleString(row.left, `${rl}.left`, 42), right: visibleString(row.right, `${rl}.right`, 42) }; }) }; break;
      }
      case 'metric-bars': {
        keys(p, ['title', 'unit', 'scaleMax', 'bars', 'note'], pl);
        const scaleMax = number(p.scaleMax, `${pl}.scaleMax`, Number.EPSILON, 1e12);
        normal.params = { title: visibleString(p.title, `${pl}.title`, 48), unit: visibleString(p.unit, `${pl}.unit`, 16), scaleMax, note: optional('note', 100), bars: array(p.bars, `${pl}.bars`, 1, 5).map((bar, index) => { const bl = `${pl}.bars[${index}]`; object(bar, bl); keys(bar, ['label','value'], bl); return { label: visibleString(bar.label, `${bl}.label`, 26), value: number(bar.value, `${bl}.value`, 0, scaleMax) }; }) }; break;
      }
      case 'quote-focus': keys(p, ['quote', 'attribution', 'title'], pl); normal.params = { quote: visibleString(p.quote, `${pl}.quote`, 110), attribution: visibleString(p.attribution, `${pl}.attribution`, 60), title: optional('title', 40) }; break;
      case 'lower-third': keys(p, ['name', 'role', 'tag', 'side'], pl); check(['left','right'].includes(p.side ?? 'left'), `${pl}.side must be left or right`); normal.params = { name: visibleString(p.name, `${pl}.name`, 24), role: optional('role', 42), tag: optional('tag', 24), side: p.side ?? 'left' }; break;
    }
    shots.push(normal);
  }
  const full = shots.filter(s => s.recipe !== 'lower-third').sort((a,b) => a.start - b.start);
  for (let i = 1; i < full.length; i++) check(full[i].start >= full[i - 1].start + full[i - 1].duration - EPS, `Full-screen shots overlap: ${full[i - 1].id} / ${full[i].id}`);
  const lowers = shots.filter(s => s.recipe === 'lower-third').sort((a,b) => a.start - b.start);
  for (let i = 1; i < lowers.length; i++) check(lowers[i].start >= lowers[i - 1].start + lowers[i - 1].duration - EPS, `Lower-thirds overlap: ${lowers[i - 1].id} / ${lowers[i].id}`);
  const captions = array(raw.captions ?? [], 'captions', 0, 1000).map((caption, index) => { const label = `captions[${index}]`; object(caption, label); keys(caption, ['id','start','duration','text'], label); return { ...timed(caption, label), text: visibleString(caption.text, `${label}.text`, width >= height ? 100 : 58) }; }).sort((a,b) => a.start - b.start);
  for (let i = 1; i < captions.length; i++) check(captions[i].start >= captions[i - 1].start + captions[i - 1].duration - EPS, `Captions overlap: ${captions[i - 1].id} / ${captions[i].id}`);
  return { schemaVersion: 1, compositionId, title, canvas: { width, height, fps, duration, transparent, captionReserve }, style, shots, captions, assets: [...assets.values()], font, gsapVersion };
}

function sourceMarkup(shot) { return shot.source ? `<p class="motion-source">来源：${html(shot.source)}</p>` : ''; }
function content(shot) {
  const p = shot.params;
  const title = value => `<h2 class="motion-title">${html(value)}</h2>`;
  const note = value => value ? `<p class="motion-note">${html(value)}</p>` : '';
  switch (shot.recipe) {
    case 'title-reveal': return `<div class="title-copy"><p class="eyebrow">${html(p.eyebrow)}</p><div class="title-mask"><h1 class="hero-title">${html(p.title)}</h1></div><i class="accent-rule"></i>${note(p.subtitle)}</div>`;
    case 'photo-pan': return `<div class="photo-grid"><div class="photo-copy">${title(p.title)}<i class="accent-rule"></i>${note(p.subtitle)}</div><div class="photo-window"><img class="pan-photo" src="${html(p.image)}" alt="${html(p.title)}" style="object-position:${p.positionX}% ${p.positionY}%"><div class="photo-corner" data-layout-ignore></div></div></div>`;
    case 'flow-steps': return `${title(p.title)}<div class="flow-grid" data-step-count="${p.items.length}">${p.items.map((item,i) => `<div class="flow-node"><span class="step-number">${String(i + 1).padStart(2,'0')}</span><h3>${html(item.label)}</h3><p>${html(item.detail)}</p>${i < p.items.length - 1 ? '<span class="flow-connector" aria-hidden="true">→</span>' : ''}</div>`).join('')}</div>${note(p.note)}`;
    case 'checklist': return `${title(p.title)}<div class="check-grid">${p.items.map(item => `<div class="check-item"><svg class="check-mark" viewBox="0 0 60 60" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="30" cy="30" r="26"></circle><path d="M17 30L26 39L44 21"></path></svg><div class="check-copy"><h3>${html(item.label)}</h3><p>${html(item.detail)}</p></div></div>`).join('')}</div>${note(p.note)}`;
    case 'comparison': return `${title(p.title)}<div class="compare-grid"><span class="compare-empty"></span><h3 class="compare-heading">${html(p.left)}</h3><h3 class="compare-heading">${html(p.right)}</h3>${p.rows.map(row => `<span class="compare-label">${html(row.label)}</span><p class="compare-cell">${html(row.left)}</p><p class="compare-cell">${html(row.right)}</p>`).join('')}</div>${note(p.note)}`;
    case 'metric-bars': return `${title(p.title)}<div class="metrics"><p class="scale-note">共同刻度：0—${p.scaleMax} ${html(p.unit)}</p>${p.bars.map(bar => `<div class="metric-row"><div class="metric-top"><span>${html(bar.label)}</span><strong>${bar.value}<small> ${html(p.unit)}</small></strong></div><div class="metric-track"><span class="metric-fill" style="width:${100 * bar.value / p.scaleMax}%"></span></div></div>`).join('')}</div>${note(p.note)}`;
    case 'quote-focus': return `${p.title ? title(p.title) : ''}<div class="quote-block"><span class="quote-mark" aria-hidden="true">“</span><blockquote>${html(p.quote)}</blockquote><i class="accent-rule"></i><p class="quote-author">${html(p.attribution)}</p></div>`;
    case 'lower-third': return `<div class="lower-panel ${p.side}"><i class="lower-accent"></i><div class="lower-copy"><p class="lower-tag">${html(p.tag)}</p><h2>${html(p.name)}</h2><p>${html(p.role)}</p></div></div>`;
  }
}

function makeHtml(description) {
  const {canvas:c, style:s} = description;
  const fontCss = description.font
    ? `@font-face{font-family:JiaVideoLocal;src:url('${description.font.relativePath}');font-display:block}body{font-family:JiaVideoLocal,sans-serif}`
    : "@font-face{font-family:JiaVideoSystem;src:local('Microsoft YaHei');font-display:block}body{font-family:JiaVideoSystem,sans-serif}";
  const scenes = description.shots.map(shot => `<section id="${shot.id}" class="clip motion-scene recipe-${shot.recipe}" data-start="${shot.start}" data-duration="${shot.duration}" data-track-index="${shot.recipe === 'lower-third' ? 10 : 0}"><div class="motion-content">${content(shot)}${sourceMarkup(shot)}</div></section>`).join('\n');
  const captions = description.captions.map(cap => `<div id="${cap.id}" class="clip motion-caption" data-start="${cap.start}" data-duration="${cap.duration}" data-track-index="20"><span>${html(cap.text)}</span></div>`).join('\n');
  return `<!doctype html>
<html lang="zh-CN"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>${html(description.title)}</title>
<link rel="stylesheet" href="assets/motion.css">
<style>:root{--width:${c.width}px;--height:${c.height}px;--u:${Math.min(c.width,c.height)/1080}px;--paper:${s.background};--ink:${s.foreground};--muted:${s.muted};--accent:${s.accent};--caption-reserve:${c.captionReserve*c.height}px}${fontCss}</style>
</head><body class="${c.width < c.height ? 'portrait' : 'landscape'}${c.transparent ? ' transparent' : ''}">
<div id="${description.compositionId}" class="motion-composition" data-composition-id="${description.compositionId}" data-start="0" data-duration="${c.duration}" data-width="${c.width}" data-height="${c.height}" data-fps="${c.fps}">
${scenes}
${captions}
</div>
<script type="application/json" id="motion-description">${jsonForHtml({ compositionId:description.compositionId, canvas:c, shots:description.shots, captions:description.captions })}</script>
<script>window.__timelines = window.__timelines || {};</script>
<script src="assets/gsap.min.js"></script>
<script src="assets/motion-runtime.js"></script>
</body></html>\n`;
}

async function main() {
  const args = cli(process.argv.slice(2));
  if (args.help) { console.log('node build-motion.mjs --input <motion.json> --output <new-directory> --gsap <local-gsap.min.js> [--font <local-font>]'); return; }
  const inputPath = path.resolve(args.input), outputPath = path.resolve(args.output);
  check(!(await exists(outputPath)), `Output already exists; choose a new version directory: ${outputPath}`);
  const gsapPath = localPath(args.gsap, process.cwd(), '--gsap'), fontPath = args.font ? localPath(args.font, process.cwd(), '--font') : null;
  const inputBytes = await fs.readFile(inputPath);
  const raw = JSON.parse(inputBytes.toString('utf8').replace(/^\uFEFF/, ''));
  const description = await validate(raw, inputPath, gsapPath, fontPath);
  const temp = `${outputPath}.building-${randomUUID()}`;
  await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(outputPath), { recursive:true });
  await fs.mkdir(path.join(temp, 'assets'), { recursive:true });
  try {
    for (const asset of description.assets) {
      const target = path.join(temp, asset.relativePath);
      await fs.copyFile(asset.originalPath, target);
      check(await sha256(target) === asset.sha256, `Asset changed during copy: ${asset.originalPath}`);
    }
    for (const [source,dest] of [['motion-runtime.js','motion-runtime.js'],['motion.css','motion.css']]) await fs.copyFile(path.join(COMPONENTS, source), path.join(temp,'assets',dest));
    await fs.writeFile(path.join(temp,'index.html'), makeHtml(description), 'utf8');
    const receipt = { schemaVersion:1, purpose:'motion-build-dependencies', generatedAt:new Date().toISOString(), node:process.version, runtime:{ name:'GSAP', version:description.gsapVersion, path:'assets/gsap.min.js', sha256:description.assets.find(a=>a.kind==='gsap-runtime').sha256 }, adapter:{ file:'assets/motion-runtime.js', sha256:await sha256(path.join(temp,'assets/motion-runtime.js')) }, stylesheet:{ file:'assets/motion.css', sha256:await sha256(path.join(temp,'assets/motion.css')) }, font:description.font ? {kind:'local-file',family:'JiaVideoLocal',file:description.font.relativePath,sha256:description.font.sha256} : {kind:'host-system',family:'JiaVideoSystem',localName:'Microsoft YaHei',portable:false}, intendedRenderer:'HyperFrames 0.7.103', offline:true, renderVerified:false };
    const manifest = { schemaVersion:1, purpose:'motion-build-assets', input:{ path:inputPath, sha256:createHash('sha256').update(inputBytes).digest('hex') }, assets:description.assets, output:{ html:'index.html', sha256:await sha256(path.join(temp,'index.html')) }, limitations:['Source labels are user-supplied provenance, not independently verified facts.', 'Build success is not render, audio, visual or user approval.'] };
    const writeJson = (name,value) => fs.writeFile(path.join(temp,name), JSON.stringify(value,null,2)+'\n', 'utf8');
    await writeJson('motion.normalized.json', {schemaVersion:1,compositionId:description.compositionId,title:description.title,canvas:description.canvas,style:description.style,shots:description.shots,captions:description.captions});
    await writeJson('asset-manifest.json', manifest);
    await writeJson('dependencies.json', receipt);
    // mkdir is exclusive even when another process creates an empty target.
    await fs.mkdir(outputPath);
    for (const entry of await fs.readdir(temp)) await fs.rename(path.join(temp, entry), path.join(outputPath, entry));
    await fs.rmdir(temp);
    console.log(JSON.stringify({ status:'BUILT_NOT_RENDERED', output:outputPath, compositionId:description.compositionId, dimensions:[description.canvas.width,description.canvas.height], duration:description.canvas.duration, recipes:[...new Set(description.shots.map(s=>s.recipe))], assets:description.assets.length },null,2));
  } catch(e) { console.error(`Incomplete new build preserved at ${temp}`); throw e; }
}
main().catch(e => { console.error(`build-motion: ${e.message}`); process.exitCode = 1; });

``````

## scripts/inspect-video.mjs

SHA256：`b7a95dd6310dcbdbe6dfa13a15b32d34e62a19c582001e77d3e8165b34ed52e3`；对应节点：qa

``````text
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { createReadStream } from 'node:fs';
import { lstat, mkdir, readFile, stat, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import path from 'node:path';

const usage = 'node inspect-video.mjs --input <local.mp4> --output <new-qa-directory> [--config <runtime.json>] [--times "0,1.2"] [--boundaries "2,4"] [--expect-width 1920] [--expect-height 1080] [--expect-fps 30] [--expect-duration 6] [--skip-loudness]';
const valuedOptions = new Set(['--input', '--output', '--config', '--times', '--boundaries', '--expect-width', '--expect-height', '--expect-fps', '--expect-duration']);
const maxSamples = 64;

function finite(value, label, minimum = 0, integer = false) {
  if (typeof value !== 'string' || !value.trim() || !Number.isFinite(Number(value))) throw new Error(`${label} must be a finite number.`);
  const number = Number(value);
  if (number < minimum || (integer && !Number.isInteger(number))) throw new Error(`${label} must be ${integer ? 'an integer' : 'a number'} >= ${minimum}.`);
  return number;
}
function localPath(value, label) {
  if (!value || value.includes('\0') || (/^[a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*:/i.test(value) && !/^[a-z]:[\\/]/i.test(value))) throw new Error(`${label} must be a local filesystem path, not a URL.`);
  return path.resolve(value);
}
function timestamps(value, label) {
  if (value === undefined) return [];
  const values = value.split(',').map(item => finite(item, label));
  if (values.length > maxSamples) throw new Error(`${label} has more than ${maxSamples} entries.`);
  return values;
}
function rate(value) {
  const [numerator, denominator = '1'] = String(value ?? '').split('/');
  const result = Number(numerator) / Number(denominator);
  return Number.isFinite(result) && result > 0 ? result : null;
}
function numberOrNull(value) {
  if (value === undefined || value === null || String(value).trim() === '') return null;
  const result = Number(value);
  return Number.isFinite(result) ? result : null;
}
async function hashFile(file) {
  const digest = createHash('sha256');
  for await (const chunk of createReadStream(file)) digest.update(chunk);
  return digest.digest('hex');
}
async function newDirectory(directory) {
  try { await lstat(directory); } catch (error) {
    if (error.code !== 'ENOENT') throw error;
    await mkdir(path.dirname(directory), { recursive: true });
    await mkdir(directory);
    return;
  }
  throw new Error(`Output already exists; choose a new QA directory: ${directory}`);
}
function parseLoudness(stderr) {
  const summary = stderr.slice(stderr.lastIndexOf('Summary:'));
  const integrated = summary.match(/\bI:\s*(-?inf|[-+\d.]+)\s+LUFS/i)?.[1];
  const peak = summary.match(/True peak:\s*Peak:\s*(-?inf|[-+\d.]+)\s+dBFS/i)?.[1];
  if (integrated === undefined || peak === undefined) throw new Error('FFmpeg ebur128 did not return integrated loudness and true peak.');
  return {
    integratedLufs: numberOrNull(integrated),
    truePeakDbtp: numberOrNull(peak),
    rawIntegrated: integrated,
    rawTruePeak: peak,
    finite: Number.isFinite(Number(integrated)) && Number.isFinite(Number(peak)),
    method: 'FFmpeg ebur128=peak=true; first audio stream; no processing or target approval',
  };
}

async function main() {
  if (process.argv.length === 3 && process.argv[2] === '--help') { console.log(usage); return; }
  const options = new Map();
  for (let i = 2; i < process.argv.length; i++) {
    const name = process.argv[i];
    if (options.has(name)) throw new Error(`Duplicate option: ${name}`);
    if (name === '--skip-loudness') { options.set(name, true); continue; }
    if (!valuedOptions.has(name) || process.argv[i + 1] === undefined || process.argv[i + 1].startsWith('--')) throw new Error(usage);
    options.set(name, process.argv[++i]);
  }
  const input = localPath(options.get('--input'), '--input');
  const output = localPath(options.get('--output'), '--output');
  if (path.extname(input).toLowerCase() !== '.mp4') throw new Error('--input must be a local .mp4 file.');
  if (!(await stat(input)).isFile()) throw new Error('--input must be a regular file.');
  const requestedTimes = timestamps(options.get('--times'), '--times');
  const boundaries = timestamps(options.get('--boundaries'), '--boundaries');
  const expected = {};
  for (const key of ['width', 'height', 'fps', 'duration']) {
    if (options.has(`--expect-${key}`)) expected[key] = finite(options.get(`--expect-${key}`), `--expect-${key}`, key === 'width' || key === 'height' ? 1 : Number.MIN_VALUE, key === 'width' || key === 'height');
  }
  const configPath = options.has('--config') ? localPath(options.get('--config'), '--config') : null;
  const config = configPath ? JSON.parse(await readFile(configPath, 'utf8')) : {};
  const ffprobe = config.tools?.ffprobeCommand ?? 'ffprobe';
  const ffmpeg = config.tools?.ffmpegCommand ?? 'ffmpeg';
  for (const [label, executable] of Object.entries({ ffprobe, ffmpeg })) {
    if (typeof executable !== 'string' || !executable.trim() || executable.includes('\0')) throw new Error(`Invalid ${label} executable in configuration.`);
  }
  await newDirectory(output);
  await mkdir(path.join(output, 'frames'));
  await mkdir(path.join(output, 'logs'));
  const report = {
    schemaVersion: 1,
    kind: 'video-file-inspection',
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    input, output, configPath,
    status: 'TECHNICAL_CHECKS_RUNNING',
    inputSha256: await hashFile(input),
    expectations: expected,
    checks: [], commands: [], samples: [],
    review: {
      framesViewed: false,
      subjectiveVisualReviewed: false,
      subjectiveAudioReviewed: false,
      factualContentReviewed: false,
      userApproved: false,
      coreAcceptanceStateUpdated: false,
      publicationStateUpdated: false,
      scope: 'Technical evidence and decoded final-file frames only. View frames and listen to the final MP4 separately; use the existing core QA/acceptance workflow.',
    },
  };
  async function run(label, command, args, requireSuccess = true) {
    const index = String(report.commands.length + 1).padStart(2, '0');
    const result = spawnSync(command, args, { encoding: 'utf8', windowsHide: true, shell: false, maxBuffer: 64 * 1024 * 1024 });
    const record = {
      label, command, args,
      exitCode: result.status,
      signal: result.signal,
      error: result.error?.message ?? null,
      stdout: path.join('logs', `${index}-${label}.stdout.txt`),
      stderr: path.join('logs', `${index}-${label}.stderr.txt`),
    };
    report.commands.push(record);
    await writeFile(path.join(output, record.stdout), result.stdout ?? '', { flag: 'wx' });
    await writeFile(path.join(output, record.stderr), result.stderr ?? '', { flag: 'wx' });
    const ok = !result.error && result.status === 0;
    if (!ok && requireSuccess) throw new Error(`${label} failed: ${result.error?.message ?? result.stderr?.slice(-2000) ?? result.signal ?? result.status}`);
    return { ...result, ok };
  }
  function check(name, ok, details) { report.checks.push({ name, ok, ...details }); }
  try {
    const probe = JSON.parse((await run('probe', ffprobe, ['-v', 'error', '-show_streams', '-show_format', '-of', 'json', input])).stdout);
    await writeFile(path.join(output, 'probe.json'), `${JSON.stringify(probe, null, 2)}\n`, { flag: 'wx' });
    const video = probe.streams?.find(stream => stream.codec_type === 'video' && !stream.disposition?.attached_pic);
    const audio = probe.streams?.filter(stream => stream.codec_type === 'audio') ?? [];
    if (!video || !Number.isInteger(video.index)) throw new Error('No primary video stream with an established stream index.');
    const fps = rate(video.avg_frame_rate) ?? rate(video.r_frame_rate);
    if (!fps || !Number.isInteger(video.width) || video.width <= 0 || !Number.isInteger(video.height) || video.height <= 0) throw new Error('Cannot establish finite positive frame rate and dimensions.');
    const videoStart = numberOrNull(video.start_time) ?? 0;
    const declaredDuration = (numberOrNull(video.duration) ?? ((numberOrNull(video.duration_ts) ?? 0) * (rate(video.time_base) ?? 0))) || null;
    const formatDuration = numberOrNull(probe.format?.duration);
    const seekDurationHint = declaredDuration ?? formatDuration;
    if (!(seekDurationHint > 0)) throw new Error('Cannot establish a finite positive video-tail seek hint.');
    // The container duration is only a seek hint. The last actual decoded video
    // PTS below determines final-frame sampling even when audio overhangs.
    const tailStart = Math.max(0, videoStart + seekDurationHint - 3);
    const tail = JSON.parse((await run('video-tail', ffprobe, ['-v', 'error', '-select_streams', String(video.index), '-read_intervals', `${tailStart}%`, '-show_frames', '-show_entries', 'frame=best_effort_timestamp_time,duration_time,pkt_duration_time', '-of', 'json', input])).stdout);
    const frames = (tail.frames ?? []).map(frame => ({
      pts: numberOrNull(frame.best_effort_timestamp_time),
      duration: numberOrNull(frame.duration_time) ?? numberOrNull(frame.pkt_duration_time),
    })).filter(frame => frame.pts !== null).sort((a, b) => a.pts - b.pts);
    if (!frames.length) throw new Error('No decoded tail frames; cannot establish the actual last video frame.');
    const last = frames.at(-1);
    const lastFrameTime = last.pts - videoStart;
    const lastFrameDuration = last.duration > 0 ? last.duration : 1 / fps;
    const duration = lastFrameTime + lastFrameDuration;
    if (!Number.isFinite(lastFrameTime) || lastFrameTime < 0 || !Number.isFinite(duration) || duration <= 0) throw new Error('Decoded video-tail timing is invalid.');
    report.media = {
      video: { index: video.index, codec: video.codec_name, width: video.width, height: video.height, fps, startTime: videoStart, declaredDuration, duration, durationMethod: 'last decoded video PTS minus video start plus final-frame duration', lastFrameTime, lastFrameAbsolutePts: last.pts, lastFrameDuration, lastFrameDurationEstimated: !(last.duration > 0) },
      audio: audio.map(stream => ({ index: stream.index, codec: stream.codec_name, sampleRate: numberOrNull(stream.sample_rate), channels: stream.channels, startTime: numberOrNull(stream.start_time), duration: numberOrNull(stream.duration) })),
      formatDuration,
    };
    check('video-timeline', true, { duration, lastFrameTime, formatDuration, declaredDuration });
    for (const key of Object.keys(expected)) {
      const actual = key === 'duration' ? duration : key === 'fps' ? fps : video[key];
      const tolerance = key === 'duration' ? 1 / fps + 0.001 : key === 'fps' ? 0.00001 : 0;
      check(`expected-${key}`, Math.abs(actual - expected[key]) <= tolerance, { expected: expected[key], actual, tolerance });
    }
    for (const [label, values] of [['--times', requestedTimes], ['--boundaries', boundaries]]) {
      if (values.some(time => time > duration + 0.000001)) throw new Error(`${label} contains a timestamp beyond the actual video duration (${duration}s).`);
    }
    const samples = new Map();
    function addSample(requested, reason) {
      const time = Math.max(0, Math.min(lastFrameTime, requested));
      const key = time.toFixed(9);
      if (!samples.has(key)) samples.set(key, { requestedTime: time, requests: [] });
      samples.get(key).requests.push({ reason, requestedTime: requested, clamped: Math.abs(time - requested) > 0.000000001 });
    }
    addSample(0, 'first-frame');
    addSample(lastFrameTime, 'last-decoded-video-frame');
    for (const time of requestedTimes) addSample(time, 'custom');
    for (const boundary of boundaries) {
      addSample(boundary - 1 / fps, `boundary-${boundary}-minus-one-frame`);
      addSample(boundary, `boundary-${boundary}`);
      addSample(boundary + 1 / fps, `boundary-${boundary}-plus-one-frame`);
    }
    if (samples.size > maxSamples) throw new Error(`The combined deduplicated sample plan exceeds ${maxSamples} frames.`);
    report.coverage = {
      wholeFileDecoded: false,
      requestedTimes, boundaries,
      samplingClock: 'Seconds relative to the primary video stream start; selection returns the next decoded frame at or after the requested time.',
      samplingLimit: maxSamples,
      sampledFrameCount: samples.size,
      vfrNotice: 'Boundary offsets use the reported average frame period. Inspect actual extracted PTS; this is not a frame-accurate edit decision list.',
      notCovered: ['Unselected video streams visually', 'Unselected audio streams subjectively', 'Subtitle/data streams', 'Subjective image quality or listening', 'Content facts or user acceptance'],
    };
    const decodedStreams = (probe.streams ?? []).filter(stream => ['video', 'audio'].includes(stream.codec_type));
    const decodeArgs = ['-hide_banner', '-nostdin', '-v', 'error', '-xerror', '-err_detect', 'explode', '-i', input];
    for (const stream of decodedStreams) decodeArgs.push('-map', `0:${stream.index}`);
    decodeArgs.push('-f', 'null', '-');
    const decode = await run('full-decode', ffmpeg, decodeArgs, false);
    report.coverage.wholeFileDecoded = decode.ok;
    report.coverage.decodedStreamIndices = decodedStreams.map(stream => stream.index);
    check('full-video-audio-decode', decode.ok, { streamIndices: report.coverage.decodedStreamIndices });
    let sampleIndex = 0;
    for (const sample of [...samples.values()].sort((a, b) => a.requestedTime - b.requestedTime)) {
      const index = String(++sampleIndex).padStart(3, '0');
      const file = path.join('frames', `${index}-${sample.requestedTime.toFixed(6)}s.png`);
      const threshold = videoStart + sample.requestedTime - 0.000001;
      const extraction = await run(`sample-${index}`, ffmpeg, ['-hide_banner', '-nostdin', '-nostats', '-n', '-copyts', '-i', input, '-map', `0:${video.index}`, '-vf', `select=gte(t\\,${threshold}),showinfo`, '-frames:v', '1', '-fps_mode', 'passthrough', '-update', '1', path.join(output, file)], false);
      const observedPts = numberOrNull(extraction.stderr?.match(/pts_time:([-+\d.eE]+)/)?.[1]);
      const actualTime = observedPts === null ? null : observedPts - videoStart;
      let fileExists = false;
      try { fileExists = (await stat(path.join(output, file))).size > 0; } catch (error) { if (error.code !== 'ENOENT') throw error; }
      const ok = extraction.ok && fileExists && actualTime !== null;
      report.samples.push({ ...sample, file, extracted: ok, actualAbsolutePts: observedPts, actualTime, sha256: fileExists ? await hashFile(path.join(output, file)) : null });
      check(`decoded-frame-${index}`, ok, { requestedTime: sample.requestedTime, actualTime, file });
    }
    if (!audio.length) {
      report.loudness = { status: 'NOT_APPLICABLE_NO_AUDIO', note: 'A silent motion fixture can pass technical checks. Narration requirements must be checked against its brief.' };
    } else if (options.has('--skip-loudness')) {
      report.loudness = { status: 'NOT_MEASURED_EXPLICITLY_SKIPPED' };
    } else {
      const measurement = await run('audio-ebur128', ffmpeg, ['-hide_banner', '-nostdin', '-nostats', '-i', input, '-map', `0:${audio[0].index}`, '-vn', '-af', 'ebur128=peak=true:framelog=verbose', '-f', 'null', '-'], false);
      if (measurement.ok) {
        report.loudness = { status: 'MEASURED', streamIndex: audio[0].index, ...parseLoudness(measurement.stderr) };
      } else {
        report.loudness = { status: 'MEASUREMENT_FAILED', streamIndex: audio[0].index };
      }
      check('audio-loudness-measured', measurement.ok, { streamIndex: audio[0].index });
    }
    const finalHash = await hashFile(input);
    check('input-unchanged', finalHash === report.inputSha256, { originalSha256: report.inputSha256, finalSha256: finalHash });
    report.status = report.checks.every(item => item.ok) ? 'TECHNICAL_CHECKS_PASSED' : 'TECHNICAL_CHECKS_FAILED';
  } catch (error) {
    report.status = 'TECHNICAL_CHECKS_FAILED';
    report.error = error.message;
  }
  report.completedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  const reportFile = path.join(output, 'inspection.json');
  await writeFile(reportFile, `${JSON.stringify(report, null, 2)}\n`, { flag: 'wx' });
  console.log(JSON.stringify({ status: report.status, report: reportFile, sampledFrameCount: report.samples.length, framesViewed: false, subjectiveAudioReviewed: false, error: report.error ?? null }, null, 2));
  if (report.status !== 'TECHNICAL_CHECKS_PASSED') process.exitCode = 1;
}

main().catch(error => { console.error(error.message); process.exitCode = 1; });

``````

## scripts/material-library.py

SHA256：`48ce529f5a5bb1ef4adc87ed1f3a20fab692cbef324617403941d49fc97b3a57`；对应节点：material

``````text
"""Jia-video's local stock library. No generation, credentials, or timeline ownership."""
import argparse
from contextlib import closing
import hashlib
import html
import json
import math
import os
import shutil
import sqlite3
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import urllib.parse
import urllib.request
import uuid
from html.parser import HTMLParser
from pathlib import Path
from fractions import Fraction

CONFIG = Path(os.environ.get('JIA_VIDEO_WORKSPACE', 'E:/开发项目/视频剪辑skill')) / 'config/video-workflow.json'
UA = 'Jia-video/3.0 (bounded selected public-media retrieval)'
if __name__ == '__main__':
    sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8')
    sys.stderr.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8')
HOSTS = {'commons.wikimedia.org', 'upload.wikimedia.org', 'images-api.nasa.gov',
         'images-assets.nasa.gov', 'mixkit.co', 'assets.mixkit.co'}

def check(condition, message):
    if not condition:
        raise ValueError(message)

def sha(path):
    with Path(path).open('rb') as stream:
        return hashlib.file_digest(stream, 'sha256').hexdigest()

def write_new(path, data):
    path = Path(path)
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with path.open('x', encoding='utf-8') as stream:
        json.dump(data, stream, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

def native(args, timeout=180):
    result = subprocess.run([str(x) for x in args], capture_output=True, text=True, encoding='utf-8', timeout=timeout)
    check(result.returncode == 0, result.stderr[-1600:] or 'Native operation failed')
    return result.stdout

class PublicRedirect(urllib.request.HTTPRedirectHandler):
    def redirect_request(self, req, fp, code, msg, headers, newurl):
        approved_url(newurl)
        return super().redirect_request(req, fp, code, msg, headers, newurl)

def approved_url(url):
    parsed = urllib.parse.urlsplit(url)
    check(parsed.scheme == 'https' and parsed.hostname in HOSTS and not parsed.username and not parsed.password
          and parsed.port in (None, 443), 'Only configured public HTTPS source hosts are supported')
    return url

def response(url):
    approved_url(url)
    return urllib.request.build_opener(PublicRedirect()).open(
        urllib.request.Request(url, headers={'User-Agent': UA}), timeout=25)

def metadata(url):
    with response(url) as stream:
        data = stream.read(2_000_001)
        check(len(data) <= 2_000_000, 'Metadata too large')
        return data.decode('utf-8')

def info(path, cfg, full_decode=True):
    data = json.loads(native([cfg['tools']['ffprobeCommand'], '-v', 'error', '-protocol_whitelist', 'file,pipe',
                              '-show_streams', '-show_format', '-of', 'json', path], 30))
    video = next((s for s in data['streams'] if s['codec_type'] == 'video'), None)
    check(video is not None, 'No video stream')
    duration = float(data['format']['duration'])
    check(math.isfinite(duration) and duration > 0, 'Invalid duration')
    if full_decode:
        native([cfg['tools']['ffmpegCommand'], '-v', 'error', '-xerror', '-protocol_whitelist', 'file,pipe',
                '-i', path, '-f', 'null', '-'], 180)
    return {'width': video['width'], 'height': video['height'], 'duration': duration,
            'codec': video['codec_name'], 'fps': video['avg_frame_rate'], 'fullDecodePassed': full_decode,
            'audioStreams': sum(s['codec_type'] == 'audio' for s in data['streams'])}

def database(root):
    root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    db = sqlite3.connect(root / 'catalog.sqlite', timeout=15)
    db.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    db.execute('CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS assets (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, sha256 TEXT UNIQUE, path TEXT, title TEXT, tags TEXT, origins TEXT, media TEXT)')
    return db

def rows(root):
    if not (root / 'catalog.sqlite').exists():
        return []
    with closing(sqlite3.connect('file:' + (root / 'catalog.sqlite').as_posix() + '?mode=ro', uri=True)) as db:
        db.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        return [unpack(row) for row in db.execute('SELECT * FROM assets ORDER BY title')]

def unpack(row):
    result = dict(row)
    for name in ['tags', 'origins', 'media']:
        result[name] = json.loads(result[name])
    return result

def origin_ok(origin):
    check(isinstance(origin, dict) and all(str(origin.get(x, '')).strip() for x in ['provider', 'title', 'sourcePage', 'license']),
          'Origin requires provider, title, sourcePage and exact license; do not infer rights from a URL')

def register(source, origin, tags, cfg, root):
    source = Path(source).resolve()
    origin_ok(origin)
    check(source.is_file() and source.stat().st_size > 0, 'Input file missing or empty')
    check(source.suffix.lower() in ['.mp4', '.webm', '.mov', '.mkv'], 'Unsupported video extension')
    check(source.stat().st_size <= cfg['materials']['maxDownloadBytes'], 'Input exceeds configured byte limit')
    digest = sha(source)
    media = info(source, cfg)
    # Recheck the source after decoding so a changing file cannot silently enter the catalog.
    check(sha(source) == digest, 'Source changed during validation')
    with closing(database(root)) as db, db:
        old = db.execute('SELECT * FROM assets WHERE sha256=?', (digest,)).fetchone()
        if old:
            record = unpack(old)
            check(Path(record['path']).is_file() and sha(record['path']) == digest,
                  'CACHE_INVALID: existing library file is missing or corrupt; preserve it for bounded repair')
            info(record['path'], cfg)
            origins = record['origins']
            if origin not in origins:
                origins.append(origin)
            merged_tags = sorted(set(record['tags'] + tags))
            db.execute('UPDATE assets SET origins=?,tags=? WHERE id=?',
                       (json.dumps(origins, ensure_ascii=False), json.dumps(merged_tags, ensure_ascii=False), record['id']))
            return {**record, 'origins': origins, 'tags': merged_tags, 'cacheReused': True, 'reviewRequired': True}
        asset_id = 'stock-' + digest[:24]
        folder = root / 'files'
        folder.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        destination = folder / (digest + source.suffix.lower())
        check(not destination.exists(), 'Unregistered destination exists; preserve it for inspection')
        with source.open('rb') as src, destination.open('xb') as dst:
            shutil.copyfileobj(src, dst)
        check(sha(destination) == digest and sha(source) == digest, 'Source or copy changed')
        db.execute('INSERT INTO assets VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',
                   (asset_id, digest, str(destination), origin['title'], json.dumps(tags, ensure_ascii=False),
                    json.dumps([origin], ensure_ascii=False), json.dumps(media)))
        return {'id': asset_id, 'sha256': digest, 'path': str(destination), 'title': origin['title'],
                'tags': tags, 'origins': [origin], 'media': media, 'cacheReused': False, 'reviewRequired': True}

class PageTags(HTMLParser):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.options, self.modals = [], []

    def handle_starttag(self, tag, attrs):
        attrs = dict(attrs)
        if tag == 'input' and attrs.get('name') == 'download-option':
            self.options.append(attrs)
        if 'data-download--modal-url-value' in attrs:
            self.modals.append(attrs)

def mixkit_candidate(page):
    check(urllib.parse.urlsplit(page).hostname == 'mixkit.co', 'Expected a Mixkit detail page')
    body = metadata(page)
    check('data-license="videoFree"' in body, 'This version is not confirmed Mixkit Free; stop rather than use Restricted')
    parser = PageTags()
    parser.feed(body)
    option = next((x for x in parser.options if 'type=720p' in x.get('value', '')), None)
    check(option, 'Normal 720p download option not found')
    parser = PageTags()
    parser.feed(metadata(urllib.parse.urljoin(page, option['value'])))
    check(parser.modals, 'Normal download modal did not provide a file')
    modal = parser.modals[0]
    title = page.rstrip('/').split('/')[-1]
    return {'provider': 'mixkit', 'id': modal.get('data-download--modal-item-id-value'), 'title': title,
            'sourcePage': page, 'license': 'Mixkit Stock Video Free License', 'licenseUrl': 'https://mixkit.co/license/#videoFree',
            'downloadUrl': modal['data-download--modal-url-value'], 'extension': '.mp4', 'reviewRequired': True}

def online_search(provider, query, limit):
    if provider == 'commons':
        params = {'action': 'query', 'generator': 'search', 'gsrsearch': 'filetype:video ' + query,
                  'gsrnamespace': 6, 'gsrlimit': limit, 'prop': 'imageinfo', 'iiprop': 'url|size|mime|extmetadata',
                  'format': 'json', 'formatversion': 2}
        data = json.loads(metadata('https://commons.wikimedia.org/w/api.php?' + urllib.parse.urlencode(params)))
        check('error' not in data, 'Commons API returned an error')
        results = []
        for item in data.get('query', {}).get('pages', []):
            media = item.get('imageinfo', [{}])[0]
            if not media.get('mime', '').startswith('video/'):
                continue
            ext = media.get('extmetadata', {})
            field = lambda name: ext.get(name, {}).get('value', '')
            suffix = Path(urllib.parse.urlsplit(media['url']).path).suffix.lower()
            results.append({'provider': 'commons', 'id': str(item['pageid']), 'title': item['title'],
                            'sourcePage': media['descriptionurl'], 'license': field('LicenseShortName'),
                            'licenseUrl': field('LicenseUrl'), 'author': html.unescape(field('Artist')),
                            'downloadUrl': media['url'], 'extension': suffix, 'bytes': media.get('size'),
                            'description': field('ImageDescription'), 'reviewRequired': True})
        return results
    params = {'q': query, 'media_type': 'video', 'page_size': limit}
    data = json.loads(metadata('https://images-api.nasa.gov/search?' + urllib.parse.urlencode(params)))
    return [{'provider': 'nasa', 'id': item['data'][0]['nasa_id'], 'title': item['data'][0]['title'],
             'sourcePage': 'https://images.nasa.gov/details/' + urllib.parse.quote(item['data'][0]['nasa_id'], safe=''),
             'description': item['data'][0].get('description', ''), 'eventOrPublishedDate': item['data'][0].get('date_created'),
             'author': 'NASA/' + item['data'][0].get('center', ''), 'license': 'NASA media guidelines; verify item-specific third-party content',
             'licenseUrl': 'https://www.nasa.gov/nasa-brand-center/images-and-media/', 'reviewRequired': True}
            for item in data.get('collection', {}).get('items', [])]

def download(candidate, cfg, root):
    origin_ok(candidate)
    provider = candidate['provider']
    check(provider in ['mixkit', 'commons', 'nasa'], 'Provider requires normal website download then local import')
    for asset in rows(root):
        if any(o.get('provider') == provider and o.get('id') == candidate.get('id') and o.get('sourcePage') == candidate['sourcePage'] for o in asset['origins']):
            return register(asset['path'], candidate, [], cfg, root)
    if provider == 'nasa':
        data = json.loads(metadata('https://images-api.nasa.gov/asset/' + urllib.parse.quote(candidate['id'], safe='')))
        urls = [x['href'] for x in data.get('collection', {}).get('items', [])]
        url = next((x for x in urls if x.endswith('~medium.mp4')), None)
        check(url, 'No official medium MP4 rendition available')
        parts = urllib.parse.urlsplit(url)
        url = urllib.parse.urlunsplit(('https', parts.netloc, urllib.parse.quote(parts.path, safe='/%~'), parts.query, ''))
        candidate = {**candidate, 'downloadUrl': url, 'extension': '.mp4', 'rendition': 'medium'}
    url = candidate['downloadUrl']
    extension = candidate['extension']
    check(extension in ['.mp4', '.webm', '.mov', '.mkv'], 'Unsupported source video type; use explicit local conversion')
    # Only the media host of the selected provider may serve its file.
    expected = {'mixkit': 'assets.mixkit.co', 'commons': 'upload.wikimedia.org', 'nasa': 'images-assets.nasa.gov'}[provider]
    check(urllib.parse.urlsplit(url).hostname == expected, 'Selected file does not match provider host')
    folder = root / 'receipts' / uuid.uuid4().hex
    folder.mkdir(parents=True)
    partial = folder / ('download.partial' + extension)
    start = time.monotonic()
    receipt = {'source': candidate, 'startedAt': time.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%z'), 'authenticated': False}
    try:
        total = 0
        with response(url) as stream:
            declared = stream.headers.get('Content-Length')
            content_type = stream.headers.get('Content-Type', '')
            check(content_type.startswith('video/') or content_type.startswith('application/octet-stream'), 'Response is not media')
            check(not declared or int(declared) <= cfg['materials']['maxDownloadBytes'], 'File exceeds configured byte limit')
            with partial.open('xb') as dest:
                while True:
                    check(time.monotonic() - start <= cfg['materials']['downloadDeadlineSeconds'], 'Download deadline exceeded')
                    chunk = stream.read(128 * 1024)
                    if not chunk:
                        break
                    total += len(chunk)
                    check(total <= cfg['materials']['maxDownloadBytes'], 'Stream exceeds configured byte limit')
                    dest.write(chunk)
            check(not declared or int(declared) == total, 'Truncated download')
            receipt.update(httpStatus=stream.status, bytes=total, contentType=content_type)
        validated = info(partial, cfg)
        complete = folder / ('source' + extension)
        partial.rename(complete)
        asset = register(complete, candidate, [], cfg, root)
        receipt.update(status='downloaded-and-validated', sha256=asset['sha256'], assetId=asset['id'], media=validated)
        return {**asset, 'downloadReceipt': str(folder / 'receipt.json')}
    except Exception as error:
        receipt.update(status='failed', error=str(error))
        raise
    finally:
        receipt['elapsedSeconds'] = round(time.monotonic() - start, 3)
        write_new(folder / 'receipt.json', receipt)

def semantic(command, cfg, cfg_path, root, query=None, limit=6):
    runtime = cfg['materials']['semantic']
    args = [runtime['pythonPath'], Path(__file__).with_name('material-videoseek.py'), command, '--config', cfg_path, '--library-root', root]
    if query:
        args += ['--query', query, '--limit', limit]
    return json.loads(native(args, 600))

def cut(asset, args, cfg, root):
    check(args.review_note and args.review_note.strip(), 'Provide a concrete review note for the selected shot')
    check(sha(asset['path']) == asset['sha256'], 'CACHE_INVALID: source hash changed')
    media = info(asset['path'], cfg)
    check(math.isfinite(args.start) and math.isfinite(args.end) and 0 <= args.start < args.end <= media['duration'], 'Invalid source interval')
    output = Path(args.output).resolve()
    check((root / 'files').resolve() not in output.parents, 'Do not write selected clips into the indexed original-file directory')
    check(output.suffix.lower() == '.mp4' and not output.exists() and not Path(str(output) + '.asset.json').exists(), 'Choose a new MP4 output and sidecar')
    check(args.fps >= 1 and args.fps <= 120 and args.width >= 16 and args.width % 2 == 0, 'Invalid output fps or even width')
    output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    partial = output.with_name(output.stem + '-' + uuid.uuid4().hex[:8] + '.partial.mp4')
    native([cfg['tools']['ffmpegCommand'], '-v', 'error', '-ss', args.start, '-i', asset['path'], '-t', args.end - args.start,
            '-vf', f'scale={args.width}:-2,fps={args.fps}', '-an', '-c:v', 'libx264', '-preset', 'fast', '-crf', '20',
            '-pix_fmt', 'yuv420p', '-movflags', '+faststart', '-n', partial])
    actual = info(partial, cfg)
    check(abs(actual['duration'] - (args.end - args.start)) <= 1.1 / args.fps, 'Actual duration differs from selected source interval')
    check(sha(asset['path']) == asset['sha256'], 'Source changed during cut')
    check(not output.exists(), 'Output appeared during render; preserve it')
    # Exclusive publication prevents a parallel exporter from being overwritten.
    os.link(partial, output)
    partial.unlink()
    result = {'kind': 'jia-video-selected-stock-clip', 'assetId': asset['id'], 'sourcePath': asset['path'],
              'sourceSha256': asset['sha256'], 'sourceIn': args.start, 'sourceOut': args.end, 'output': str(output),
              'outputSha256': sha(output), 'media': actual, 'origins': asset['origins'], 'reviewNote': args.review_note,
              'transformations': ['source-interval-selection', 'scaled', 'reencoded', 'muted'], 'userAcceptance': 'pending'}
    write_new(str(output) + '.asset.json', result)
    return result

def run(args):
    cfg_path = Path(args.config).resolve()
    cfg = json.loads(cfg_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))
    root = Path(args.library_root or cfg['materials']['libraryRoot']).resolve()
    command = args.command
    if command == 'doctor':
        runtime = cfg['materials']['semantic']
        return {'kind': 'jia-video-material-doctor', 'libraryRoot': str(root), 'registeredAssets': len(rows(root)),
                'publicProviders': ['commons', 'nasa', 'mixkit-detail-page'], 'networkRequests': 0,
                'semanticRuntimeReady': all(Path(runtime[x]).exists() for x in ['pythonPath', 'sourceRoot', 'modelsRoot']),
                'semanticIndexExists': (root / 'index-data').exists(), 'reviewRequired': True}
    if command == 'import':
        origin = json.loads(Path(args.origin).read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))
        return register(args.input, origin, args.tags or [], cfg, root)
    if command == 'download':
        check(args.license_review and args.license_review.strip(), 'Record intended usage and license review before download or cache adoption')
        if args.page:
            cached = next((a for a in rows(root) if any(o.get('provider') == 'mixkit' and o.get('sourcePage') == args.page for o in a['origins'])), None)
            if cached:
                origin = next(o for o in cached['origins'] if o.get('sourcePage') == args.page)
                return register(cached['path'], {**origin, 'licenseReview': args.license_review}, [], cfg, root)
            candidate = mixkit_candidate(args.page)
        else:
            check(args.input and args.id, 'Use a selected Mixkit --page or a saved candidate list --input and --id')
            records = json.loads(Path(args.input).read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))
            candidates = records['candidates']
            candidate = next((x for x in candidates if x['id'] == args.id), None)
            check(candidate is not None, 'Candidate ID not found')
        check(args.license_review and args.license_review.strip(), 'Record the exact intended usage and license review before download')
        candidate['licenseReview'] = args.license_review
        return download(candidate, cfg, root)
    if command == 'index':
        for asset in rows(root):
            check(sha(asset['path']) == asset['sha256'], 'CACHE_INVALID: check library before indexing')
        return semantic('index', cfg, cfg_path, root)
    if command == 'search':
        if args.provider in ['commons', 'nasa']:
            results = online_search(args.provider, args.query, args.limit)
        elif args.provider == 'semantic':
            response_data = semantic('search', cfg, cfg_path, root, args.query, args.limit)
            known = {str(Path(a['path']).resolve()).casefold(): a for a in rows(root)}
            results = []
            for hit in response_data.get('hits', []):
                asset = known.get(str(Path(hit['video_path']).resolve()).casefold())
                if asset:
                    results.append({**hit, 'assetId': asset['id'], 'origins': asset['origins'], 'reviewRequired': True})
        else:
            terms = args.query.casefold().split()
            results = [a for a in rows(root) if all(t in (a['title'] + ' ' + ' '.join(a['tags'])).casefold() for t in terms)]
            results = results[:args.limit]
        return {'provider': args.provider, 'query': args.query, 'candidates': results,
                'reviewRequired': True, 'noAutomaticAdoption': True}
    assets = rows(root)
    if command == 'list':
        return {'assets': assets}
    asset = next((x for x in assets if x['id'] == args.id), None)
    check(asset is not None, 'Unknown library asset ID')
    if command == 'verify':
        check(sha(asset['path']) == asset['sha256'], 'CACHE_INVALID: SHA mismatch')
        return {**asset, 'media': info(asset['path'], cfg), 'verified': True}
    return cut(asset, args, cfg, root)

def parser():
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    p.add_argument('--config', default=str(CONFIG))
    p.add_argument('--library-root')
    sub = p.add_subparsers(dest='command', required=True)
    for name in ['doctor', 'list', 'index']:
        sub.add_parser(name)
    s = sub.add_parser('search')
    s.add_argument('--provider', choices=['local', 'semantic', 'commons', 'nasa'], default='local')
    s.add_argument('--query', required=True)
    s.add_argument('--limit', type=int, choices=range(1, 11), default=6)
    i = sub.add_parser('import')
    i.add_argument('--input', required=True)
    i.add_argument('--origin', required=True)
    i.add_argument('--tags', nargs='*')
    d = sub.add_parser('download')
    d.add_argument('--page')
    d.add_argument('--input')
    d.add_argument('--id')
    d.add_argument('--license-review', required=True)
    v = sub.add_parser('verify')
    v.add_argument('--id', required=True)
    c = sub.add_parser('cut')
    for key in ['id', 'output', 'review-note']:
        c.add_argument('--' + key, required=True)
    c.add_argument('--start', required=True, type=float)
    c.add_argument('--end', required=True, type=float)
    c.add_argument('--fps', type=Fraction, default=30)
    c.add_argument('--width', type=int, default=1280)
    return p

if __name__ == '__main__':
    try:
        result = run(parser().parse_args())
        print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False))
    except Exception as error:
        print(json.dumps({'ok': False, 'error': str(error)}, ensure_ascii=False), file=sys.stderr)
        sys.exit(1)

``````

## scripts/material-videoseek.py

SHA256：`e55ae3d599a15dfbdec3e452a87bc14b90a276437bf61a0bf6198521a2480884`；对应节点：material

``````text
"""Process-isolated offline VideoSeek adapter; no GUI, cloud, resident service or second timeline."""
import argparse
import contextlib
import copy
import json
import os
import shutil
import sys
from pathlib import Path

if __name__ == '__main__':
    sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8')
    sys.stderr.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8')

def run(args):
    cfg = json.loads(Path(args.config).read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))
    runtime = cfg['materials']['semantic']
    root = Path(args.library_root).resolve()
    data_root = root / 'index-data'
    os.environ.update(LOCALAPPDATA=str(data_root), HF_HOME=str(data_root / 'hf-cache'), HF_HUB_OFFLINE='1',
                      TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE='1', VIDEOSEEK_INFERENCE_EP='cpu', VIDEOSEEK_AGENT_EXPORT_STRICT='1')
    sys.path.insert(0, runtime['sourceRoot'])
    from src.app.config import DEFAULT_CONFIG, CONFIG_FILE, build_data_storage_paths, load_config, save_config
    config_path = Path(CONFIG_FILE).resolve()
    if data_root not in config_path.parents:
        raise ValueError('Refusing to use default user VideoSeek configuration')
    if args.command == 'index':
        local = copy.deepcopy(DEFAULT_CONFIG)
        local.update(schema_version=2, models={'active_profile': '', 'profiles': []}, model_dir=runtime['modelsRoot'],
                     data_root=str(data_root / 'VideoSeek'), prefer_gpu=False, inference_ep='cpu', embedding_batch_size=4,
                     sampling_fps_mode='fixed', fps=1, indexing_video_workers=1, agent_api_enabled=False,
                     search_telemetry_enabled=False, text_search_enhance_enabled=False, frame_neighbor_rerank_enabled=False,
                     search_video_discovery_enabled=False, ffmpeg_path=shutil.which(cfg['tools']['ffmpegCommand']))
        local.update(build_data_storage_paths(local['data_root']))
        for profile in local['understanding']['profiles']:
            profile['enabled'] = False
        config_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        if not config_path.exists():
            config_path.write_text(json.dumps(local, ensure_ascii=False), encoding='utf-8')
        from src.services.model_package_service import import_model_packages
        import_model_packages(runtime['modelsRoot'])
        local = load_config()
        local['models']['active_profile'] = 'chinese_clip_vit_base_patch16'
``````
