``````text
        for profile in local['models']['profiles']:
            profile['runtime']['prefer_gpu'] = False
        save_config(local)
        from src.services.library_service import add_library, register_library_videos
        library = str(root / 'files')
        if not Path(library).is_dir():
            raise ValueError('Import or download licensed library files before indexing')
        add_library(library)
        register_library_videos(config=local, library_path=library)
        from src.storage.asset_store import load_model_metadata, save_model_metadata
        from src.storage.video_identity import get_video_hash
        from src.services.indexing_service import scan_target_libraries
        meta = load_model_metadata(config=local)
        issues = []
        result = scan_target_libraries(meta, local, get_video_hash, target_lib=library,
                                       persist_meta_callback=lambda: save_model_metadata(meta, config=local),
                                       issue_callback=lambda *a, **k: issues.append(str(a)))
        save_model_metadata(meta, config=local)
        if issues or not result[1]:
            raise ValueError('Indexing incomplete: ' + str(issues) + ' ' + str(result))
        return {'kind': 'jia-video-local-semantic-index', 'library': library, 'result': result,
                'offline': True, 'reviewRequired': True, 'cloudProvidersUsed': []}
    if not config_path.exists():
        raise ValueError('Run materials index before semantic search')
    from src.web.agent_api.schemas import AgentSearchRequest, AgentSearchScope
    from src.web.agent_api.search import execute_agent_search
    request = AgentSearchRequest(query=args.query, query_type='text', mode='frame', top_k=args.limit,
                                 text_enhance=False, search_precision_mode='fast',
                                 scope=AgentSearchScope(library_paths=[str(root / 'files')]),
                                 expand_frame_hits=True, pad_before_sec=1.5, pad_after_sec=1.5)
    return execute_agent_search(request)

if __name__ == '__main__':
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    p.add_argument('command', choices=['index', 'search'])
    p.add_argument('--config', required=True)
    p.add_argument('--library-root', required=True)
    p.add_argument('--query')
    p.add_argument('--limit', type=int, default=6)
    try:
        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(sys.stderr):
            result = run(p.parse_args())
        print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False))
    except Exception as error:
        print(json.dumps({'ok': False, 'error': str(error)}, ensure_ascii=False), file=sys.stderr)
        sys.exit(1)

``````

## scripts/normalize-audio.mjs

SHA256：`6f10ff5d62de6d8e6f6ee03f4ef12ab4e1b2248076fb26f4df1080aa853b99cb`；对应节点：qa

``````text
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { copyFile, mkdir, readFile, stat, unlink, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { constants, createReadStream } from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { randomUUID, createHash } from 'node:crypto';

function run(command, args) {
  const result = spawnSync(command, args, { encoding: 'utf8', windowsHide: true, maxBuffer: 16 * 1024 * 1024 });
  if (result.error || result.status !== 0) throw new Error(result.error?.message ?? result.stderr?.slice(-4000) ?? 'Media command failed.');
  return result;
}
async function hashFile(file) {
  const digest = createHash('sha256');
  for await (const chunk of createReadStream(file)) digest.update(chunk);
  return digest.digest('hex');
}
async function absent(file) {
  try { await stat(file); } catch (error) { if (error.code === 'ENOENT') return; throw error; }
  throw new Error(`Output already exists; use a new version: ${file}`);
}
function measurements(stderr) {
  const match = stderr.match(/\{\s*"input_i"[\s\S]*?\}/);
  if (!match) throw new Error('FFmpeg did not return loudnorm measurements.');
  const values = JSON.parse(match[0]);
  for (const key of ['input_i','input_tp','input_lra','input_thresh','target_offset']) {
    if (!Number.isFinite(Number(values[key]))) throw new Error(`Non-finite loudness measurement (${key}); inspect the source audio instead of normalizing silence.`);
  }
  return values;
}
async function main() {
  const options = new Map();
  for (let i = 2; i < process.argv.length; i += 2) {
    if (!['--input','--output','--config'].includes(process.argv[i]) || !process.argv[i + 1]) throw new Error('Use --input <mp4> --output <new.mp4> [--config <runtime.json>].');
    if (options.has(process.argv[i])) throw new Error(`Duplicate option: ${process.argv[i]}`);
    options.set(process.argv[i], process.argv[i + 1]);
  }
  if (!options.get('--input') || !options.get('--output')) throw new Error('--input and --output are required.');
  const input = path.resolve(options.get('--input'));
  const output = path.resolve(options.get('--output'));
  if (input.toLowerCase() === output.toLowerCase() || path.extname(output).toLowerCase() !== '.mp4') throw new Error('Use a different new .mp4 output path.');
  const receipt = `${output}.loudness.json`;
  await absent(output);
  await absent(receipt);
  if (!(await stat(input)).isFile()) throw new Error('Input must be a media file.');
  const config = options.get('--config') ? JSON.parse(await readFile(options.get('--config'), 'utf8')) : {};
  const ffmpeg = config.tools?.ffmpegCommand ?? 'ffmpeg';
  const ffprobe = config.tools?.ffprobeCommand ?? 'ffprobe';
  const probe = JSON.parse(run(ffprobe, ['-v','error','-show_streams','-show_format','-of','json',input]).stdout);
  const sourceVideo = probe.streams?.find(s => s.codec_type === 'video' && !s.disposition?.attached_pic);
  const sourceAudio = probe.streams?.find(s => s.codec_type === 'audio');
  if (!sourceVideo || !sourceAudio) throw new Error('Normalization requires video and an existing audio track; it does not invent narration.');
  if (!Number.isInteger(sourceVideo.index) || !Number.isInteger(sourceAudio.index)) throw new Error('Cannot establish selected video/audio stream indices.');
  const duration = Number(sourceVideo.duration ?? probe.format?.duration);
  const sourceAudioDuration = Number(sourceAudio.duration ?? probe.format?.duration);
  const [rateNumerator, rateDenominator = '1'] = String(sourceVideo.avg_frame_rate ?? sourceVideo.r_frame_rate).split('/');
  const frameRate = Number(rateNumerator) / Number(rateDenominator);
  if (!Number.isFinite(duration) || duration <= 0 || !Number.isFinite(frameRate) || frameRate <= 0 || !Number.isFinite(sourceAudioDuration)) throw new Error('Cannot establish the source video timeline duration and frame rate.');
  if (Math.abs(sourceAudioDuration - duration) > 1 / frameRate + 0.001) throw new Error('Source audio/video durations already differ by more than one frame; inspect alignment before loudness processing.');
  const sourceHash = await hashFile(input);
  const first = run(ffmpeg, ['-hide_banner','-nostats','-i',input,'-map','0:a:0','-af','loudnorm=I=-16:TP=-1:LRA=11:print_format=json','-f','null',process.platform === 'win32' ? 'NUL' : '/dev/null']);
  const measured = measurements(first.stderr);
  let gainDb = -16 - Number(measured.input_i);
  const limiterCeilingDb = -2;
  const limiterLinear = 10 ** (limiterCeilingDb / 20);
  // Measure loudness without applying loudnorm's dynamic output: it shifted
  // late speech events in the real forward test. Static gain + a compensated
  // lookahead limiter preserves event positions; oversampling controls peaks.
  const filterForGain = value => `volume=${value}dB,aresample=192000,alimiter=limit=${limiterLinear}:attack=5:release=50:level=false:latency=true,aresample=48000,apad=whole_dur=${duration},atrim=duration=${duration}`;
  await mkdir(path.dirname(output), { recursive: true });
  const temporary = path.join(path.dirname(output), `.${path.basename(output, '.mp4')}.${randomUUID()}.mp4`);
  let temporaryCreated = false;
  try {
    temporaryCreated = true;
    const passes = [];
    let finalMeasurements;
    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 2; attempt++) {
      run(ffmpeg, ['-hide_banner','-nostats','-n','-i',input,'-map',`0:${sourceVideo.index}`,'-map',`0:${sourceAudio.index}`,'-map_metadata','0','-c:v','copy','-c:a','aac','-b:a','192k','-ar','48000','-af',filterForGain(gainDb),'-t',String(duration),'-movflags','+faststart',temporary]);
      finalMeasurements = measurements(run(ffmpeg, ['-hide_banner','-nostats','-i',temporary,'-map','0:a:0','-af','loudnorm=I=-16:TP=-1:LRA=11:print_format=json','-f','null',process.platform === 'win32' ? 'NUL' : '/dev/null']).stderr);
      const integrated = Number(finalMeasurements.input_i);
      const truePeak = Number(finalMeasurements.input_tp);
      passes.push({ gainDb, integratedLufs: integrated, truePeakDb: truePeak });
      if (integrated >= -17 && integrated <= -15 && truePeak <= -1) break;
      const correction = -16 - integrated;
      if (attempt === 1 || truePeak > -1 || Math.abs(correction) > 3) throw new Error(`Audio needs manual mixing after gain/limiting (LUFS ${integrated}, dBTP ${truePeak}); no output version was committed.`);
      // At most one measured correction. Only this invocation's UUID file
      // is removed; original media and existing outputs are never changed.
      await unlink(temporary);
      gainDb += correction;
    }
    if (await hashFile(input) !== sourceHash) throw new Error('Input changed while normalizing; no output version was committed.');
    const processed = JSON.parse(run(ffprobe, ['-v','error','-show_streams','-show_format','-of','json',temporary]).stdout);
    const processedVideo = processed.streams?.find(s => s.codec_type === 'video' && !s.disposition?.attached_pic);
    const processedAudio = processed.streams?.find(s => s.codec_type === 'audio');
    const outputVideoDuration = Number(processedVideo?.duration ?? processed.format?.duration);
    const outputAudioDuration = Number(processedAudio?.duration ?? processed.format?.duration);
    if (!Number.isFinite(outputVideoDuration) || !Number.isFinite(outputAudioDuration) || Math.abs(outputVideoDuration - duration) > 1 / frameRate + 0.001 || Math.abs(outputAudioDuration - outputVideoDuration) > 1 / frameRate + 0.001) throw new Error('Normalized output did not preserve the source timeline; no output version was committed.');
    await copyFile(temporary, output, constants.COPYFILE_EXCL);
    const outputHash = await hashFile(output);
    const result = { input, sourceSha256: sourceHash, output, outputSha256: outputHash, sourceMeasurements: measured, outputMeasurements: finalMeasurements, method: { kind: 'measured-static-gain-and-latency-compensated-oversampled-limiter', gainDb, limiterCeilingDb, passes }, timeline: { sourceVideoDuration: duration, sourceAudioDuration, outputVideoDuration, outputAudioDuration, frameRate }, target: { integratedLufs: -16, truePeakDb: -1, audioSampleRate: 48000 }, videoReencoded: false, finalQaRequired: true, subjectiveAudioReviewed: false };
    await writeFile(receipt, `${JSON.stringify(result, null, 2)}\n`, { flag: 'wx' });
    process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify({ output, receipt, outputSha256: outputHash, finalQaRequired: true })}\n`);
  } finally {
    if (temporaryCreated) await unlink(temporary).catch(error => { if (error.code !== 'ENOENT') throw error; });
  }
}
main().catch(error => { process.stderr.write(`${error.message}\n`); process.exitCode = 1; });

``````

## scripts/render-data-chart.mjs

SHA256：`a951b6e481a6a1c0802398cf673301eefc0668191003b78a65d3761610a1afa2`；对应节点：chart

``````text
#!/usr/bin/env node
import fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import path from 'node:path';
import {createHash} from 'node:crypto';
import {createRequire} from 'node:module';
import {fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL} from 'node:url';

const DEFAULT_CONFIG = 'E:\\开发项目\\视频剪辑skill\\config\\video-workflow.json';
const TEMPLATE = fileURLToPath(new URL('../assets/livecanvas-remotion/', import.meta.url));
const EXPECTED = {'@remotion/bundler':'4.0.530', '@remotion/renderer':'4.0.530', remotion:'4.0.530', react:'19.1.0', 'react-dom':'19.1.0'};
const FRAMES = [0, 30, 60, 78, 89];
const HELP = `Local LiveCanvas data chart renderer (fixed 1080x1440 / 30fps / 90 frames).
  node render-data-chart.mjs --data <json> --output <NEW directory> [--allow-demo]
       [--config <runtime-json>] [--runtime <installed project>] [--browser <local executable>]
  node render-data-chart.mjs --doctor [--config <runtime-json>] [--runtime <project>] [--browser <executable>]
No dependency install, browser download, remote media, audio, or Live Photo pairing.
Synthetic data requires explicit --allow-demo. Real data requires HTTP(S) source URLs.
An existing output directory is always rejected; failed engineering remains for inspection.`;

const fail = (message) => {throw new Error(message);};
const object = (value, label) => {
  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value)) fail(`${label}: expected object`);
};
const textField = (value, label) => {
  if (typeof value !== 'string' || !value.trim()) fail(`${label}: expected nonempty text`);
};
const date = (value, label) => {
  if (typeof value !== 'string' || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value)) fail(`${label}: expected YYYY-MM-DD`);
  const ms = Date.parse(`${value}T00:00:00Z`);
  if (!Number.isFinite(ms) || new Date(ms).toISOString().slice(0,10) !== value) fail(`${label}: invalid calendar date`);
  return ms;
};
const sha = (bytes) => createHash('sha256').update(bytes).digest('hex');
const readJson = async (file) => JSON.parse((await fs.readFile(file,'utf8')).replace(/^\uFEFF/,''));
const exists = async (file) => {
  try {await fs.lstat(file); return true;} catch (error) {if(error.code === 'ENOENT') return false; throw error;}
};

function args(argv) {
  const out = {};
  const values = new Set(['data','output','config','runtime','browser']);
  const switches = new Set(['doctor','allow-demo','help']);
  for (let i=0; i<argv.length; i++) {
    const name = argv[i].startsWith('--') ? argv[i].slice(2) : '';
    if (!values.has(name) && !switches.has(name)) fail(`Unknown argument: ${argv[i]}`);
    if (Object.hasOwn(out,name)) fail(`Duplicate argument: --${name}`);
    if (values.has(name)) {
      const value = argv[++i];
      if (!value || value.startsWith('--')) fail(`Missing value: --${name}`);
      out[name] = path.resolve(value);
    } else out[name] = true;
  }
  if (out.help) {
    if (Object.keys(out).length !== 1) fail('--help must be used alone');
    return out;
  }
  if (out.doctor) {
    if (out.data || out.output || out['allow-demo']) fail('--doctor only accepts --config, --runtime, and --browser');
  } else if (!out.data || !out.output) fail('Required: --data and --output (or --doctor / --help)');
  return out;
}

function validate(data, allowDemo) {
  object(data,'data');
  if (data.schemaVersion !== 1) fail('data.schemaVersion must be 1');
  if (typeof data.demo !== 'boolean') fail('data.demo must be boolean');
  textField(data.provenance,'data.provenance');
  const synthetic = data.demo || data.provenance === 'synthetic';
  if (synthetic && !allowDemo) fail('Synthetic data requires explicit --allow-demo');
  for (const key of ['title','subtitle','footnote']) textField(data[key],`data.${key}`);
  date(data.asOf,'data.asOf');
  object(data.metric,'data.metric');
  for (const key of ['kind','label','unit']) textField(data.metric[key],`data.metric.${key}`);
  object(data.period,'data.period');
  const start = date(data.period.start,'data.period.start');
  const end = date(data.period.end,'data.period.end');
  if (!(start < end)) fail('period.start must be earlier than period.end');
  if (!['daily','monthly','yearly'].includes(data.period.granularity)) fail('period.granularity must be daily/monthly/yearly');
  for (const key of ['timezone','definition']) textField(data.period[key],`data.period.${key}`);
  object(data.display,'data.display');
  const domain = data.display.yDomain;
  if (!Array.isArray(domain) || domain.length !== 2 || !domain.every(Number.isFinite) || !(domain[0] < domain[1])) fail('display.yDomain requires two finite increasing numbers');
  if (!Number.isInteger(data.display.decimals) || data.display.decimals < 0 || data.display.decimals > 4) fail('display.decimals must be an integer from 0 through 4');
  if (!Array.isArray(data.sources) || !data.sources.length) fail('data.sources must be a nonempty array');
  const sources = new Set();
  for (const [i,source] of data.sources.entries()) {
    object(source,`sources[${i}]`);
    for (const key of ['id','title','url']) textField(source[key],`sources[${i}].${key}`);
    if (sources.has(source.id)) fail(`Duplicate source id: ${source.id}`);
    sources.add(source.id);
    let url;
    try {url = new URL(source.url);} catch {fail(`sources[${i}].url: invalid URL`);}
    if (!synthetic && !['http:','https:'].includes(url.protocol)) fail(`sources[${i}].url: real data requires HTTP(S)`);
    if (source.accessedAt !== undefined) date(source.accessedAt,`sources[${i}].accessedAt`);
    if (source.method !== undefined) textField(source.method,`sources[${i}].method`);
  }
  if (!Array.isArray(data.series) || data.series.length < 1 || data.series.length > 4) fail('data.series requires 1 through 4 series');
  const ids = new Set();
  for (const [i,series] of data.series.entries()) {
    object(series,`series[${i}]`);
    for (const key of ['id','name','color']) textField(series[key],`series[${i}].${key}`);
    if (ids.has(series.id)) fail(`Duplicate series id: ${series.id}`);
    ids.add(series.id);
    // Colors enter SVG/CSS only as colors: URL values and arbitrary CSS are not supported.
    if (!/^#[\da-f]{6}$/i.test(series.color)) fail(`series[${i}].color: expected six-digit hex color`);
    if (!Array.isArray(series.points) || !series.points.length) fail(`series[${i}].points must be nonempty`);
    let previous = -Infinity;
    let observed = 0;
    for (const [j,point] of series.points.entries()) {
      object(point,`series[${i}].points[${j}]`);
      const ms = date(point.date,`series[${i}].points[${j}].date`);
      if (!(ms > previous)) fail(`series[${i}] dates must be strictly increasing and unique`);
      if (ms < start || ms > end) fail(`series[${i}].points[${j}].date falls outside period`);
      previous = ms;
      textField(point.sourceId,`series[${i}].points[${j}].sourceId`);
      if (!sources.has(point.sourceId)) fail(`Unknown point sourceId: ${point.sourceId}`);
      if (point.value !== null) {
        if (!Number.isFinite(point.value)) fail(`series[${i}].points[${j}].value must be finite or null`);
        if (point.value < domain[0] || point.value > domain[1]) fail(`series[${i}].points[${j}].value lies outside yDomain`);
        observed++;
      }
    }
    if (!observed) fail(`series[${i}] needs at least one observed value (the template requires an observed first series)`);
  }
  const renderData = structuredClone(data);
  if (synthetic) renderData.demo = true;
  return {synthetic, renderData};
}

async function packageInfo(req, name) {
  const entry = req.resolve(name);
  let dir = path.dirname(entry);
  for (;;) {
    const file = path.join(dir,'package.json');
    if (await exists(file)) {
      const pkg = await readJson(file);
      if (pkg.name === name) return {name,version:pkg.version,entry,packageJson:file};
    }
    const parent = path.dirname(dir);
    if (parent === dir) fail(`Cannot locate installed package metadata: ${name}`);
    dir = parent;
  }
}

async function runtimeState(options) {
  const configPath = options.config || DEFAULT_CONFIG;
  const state = {configPath,runtimeProject:null,browserPath:null,packages:[],errors:[],ready:false,networkInstall:false};
  let config = {};
  try {config = await readJson(configPath);} catch (error) {
    // Explicit runtime AND browser make configuration optional; do not hide malformed JSON.
    if (!(error.code === 'ENOENT' && options.runtime && options.browser)) state.errors.push(`Configuration: ${error.message}`);
  }
  state.runtimeProject = options.runtime || config.dataCharts?.runtimeProject || null;
  state.browserPath = options.browser || config.render?.browserPath || null;
  if (state.runtimeProject) state.runtimeProject = path.resolve(state.runtimeProject);
  if (state.browserPath) state.browserPath = path.resolve(state.browserPath);
  if (!state.browserPath) state.errors.push('Set config.render.browserPath or --browser to an existing local browser');
  else {
    try {if (!(await fs.stat(state.browserPath)).isFile()) fail('not a file');}
    catch (error) {state.errors.push(`Browser: ${error.message}`);}
  }
  if (!state.runtimeProject) state.errors.push('Set config.dataCharts.runtimeProject or --runtime; no fallback to other Remotion projects');
  else {
    try {
      const runtimePackage = path.join(state.runtimeProject,'package.json');
      await fs.access(runtimePackage);
      const nodeModules = path.join(state.runtimeProject,'node_modules');
      if (!(await fs.stat(nodeModules)).isDirectory()) fail('node_modules is not a directory');
      const req = createRequire(runtimePackage);
      for (const [name,expected] of Object.entries(EXPECTED)) {
        try {
          const info = await packageInfo(req,name);
          state.packages.push({...info,expected});
          if (info.version !== expected) state.errors.push(`${name}: installed ${info.version}, expected ${expected}`);
          const relative = path.relative(nodeModules,info.entry);
          if (relative.startsWith('..') || path.isAbsolute(relative)) state.errors.push(`${name}: resolves outside selected runtime node_modules`);
        } catch (error) {state.errors.push(`${name}: ${error.message}`);}
      }
    } catch (error) {state.errors.push(`Runtime: ${error.message}`);}
  }
  state.ready = state.errors.length === 0;
  return state;
}

async function fileTree(root, prefix='') {
  const files = [];
  for (const item of (await fs.readdir(path.join(root,prefix),{withFileTypes:true})).sort((a,b)=>a.name.localeCompare(b.name))) {
    const relative = path.join(prefix,item.name);
    if (item.isSymbolicLink()) fail(`Template must not contain links: ${relative}`);
    if (item.isDirectory()) files.push(...await fileTree(root,relative));
    else if (item.isFile()) {
      const bytes = await fs.readFile(path.join(root,relative));
      files.push({path:relative.replaceAll('\\','/'),bytes:bytes.length,sha256:sha(bytes)});
    } else fail(`Unsupported template entry: ${relative}`);
  }
  return files;
}

async function main() {
  const options = args(process.argv.slice(2));
  if (options.help) {console.log(HELP); return;}
  if (options.doctor) {
    const state = await runtimeState(options);
    console.log(JSON.stringify({kind:'livecanvas-runtime-doctor',...state},null,2));
    if (!state.ready) process.exitCode = 1;
    return;
  }
  const inputBytes = await fs.readFile(options.data);
  const originalData = JSON.parse(inputBytes.toString('utf8').replace(/^\uFEFF/,''));
  const {synthetic,renderData} = validate(originalData,Boolean(options['allow-demo']));
  if (await exists(options.output)) fail(`Output directory already exists: ${options.output}`);
  const state = await runtimeState(options);
  if (!state.ready) fail(`Runtime is not ready: ${state.errors.join('; ')}`);
  const templateFiles = await fileTree(TEMPLATE);
  const receipt = {schemaVersion:1,kind:'livecanvas-local-render',status:'preparing',startedAt:new Date().toISOString(),
    outputDirectory:options.output,input:{path:options.data,sha256:sha(inputBytes),originalData},
    synthetic,syntheticLabel:synthetic?'合成示例 · 非真实数据':null,sourcesVerified:false,
    sourceVerification:'Source identifiers and URLs checked for structure only; facts were not verified.',
    geometry:{width:1080,height:1440,fps:30,durationInFrames:90,durationSeconds:3},
    template:{root:TEMPLATE,files:templateFiles},runtime:state,adapterChanges:[],artifacts:[],
    audio:false,livePhotoPairing:false,dependencyInstall:false,browserDownload:false,remoteMedia:false};
  await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(options.output),{recursive:true});
  await fs.mkdir(options.output); // exclusive: a competing run cannot reuse it
  const receiptPath = path.join(options.output,'render.json');
  try {
    await fs.writeFile(receiptPath,JSON.stringify(receipt,null,2),{flag:'wx'});
    // The root is our exclusive directory containing the initial receipt. Copy
    // children into absent destinations: fs.cp(errorOnExist) rejects the root itself.
    for (const item of await fs.readdir(TEMPLATE,{withFileTypes:true})) {
      await fs.cp(path.join(TEMPLATE,item.name),path.join(options.output,item.name),{recursive:true,errorOnExist:true,force:false});
    }
    await fs.writeFile(path.join(options.output,'data.json'),JSON.stringify(renderData,null,2)+'\n');
    receipt.adapterChanges.push({file:'data.json',change:'Replace copied example with validated input; force visible demo label for synthetic provenance.'});
    const entryPoint = path.join(options.output,'src','index.tsx');
    const source = await fs.readFile(entryPoint,'utf8');
    const fontNeedle = 'fontFamily:\'"PingFang SC", "Noto Sans CJK SC", sans-serif\'';
    if (!source.includes(fontNeedle)) fail('Pinned template no longer matches font adapter patch');
    const adapted = source.replace(fontNeedle,'fontFamily:\'"Microsoft YaHei", "PingFang SC", "Noto Sans CJK SC", sans-serif\'');
    await fs.writeFile(entryPoint,adapted);
    receipt.adapterChanges.push({file:'src/index.tsx',change:'Add Windows Microsoft YaHei font fallback.',originalSha256:sha(Buffer.from(source)),workingSha256:sha(Buffer.from(adapted))});
    const modules = path.join(options.output,'node_modules');
    await fs.symlink(path.join(state.runtimeProject,'node_modules'),modules,process.platform==='win32'?'junction':'dir');
    receipt.runtime.nodeModulesLink = {path:modules,target:await fs.realpath(modules),type:process.platform==='win32'?'junction':'symlink'};
    receipt.status = 'rendering';
    await fs.writeFile(receiptPath,JSON.stringify(receipt,null,2));
    const req = createRequire(path.join(state.runtimeProject,'package.json'));
    const bundler = await import(pathToFileURL(req.resolve('@remotion/bundler')).href);
    const renderer = await import(pathToFileURL(req.resolve('@remotion/renderer')).href);
    const serveUrl = await bundler.bundle({entryPoint,outDir:path.join(options.output,'bundle')});
    const inputProps = {data:renderData};
    const browserExecutable = state.browserPath;
    const composition = await renderer.selectComposition({serveUrl,id:'LiveCanvas',inputProps,browserExecutable});
    if (composition.width!==1080 || composition.height!==1440 || composition.fps!==30 || composition.durationInFrames!==90) fail('Template composition differs from fixed 1080x1440 / 30fps / 90-frame contract');
    const common = {composition,serveUrl,inputProps,browserExecutable};
    await renderer.renderMedia({...common,codec:'h264',pixelFormat:'yuv420p',crf:16,concurrency:2,outputLocation:path.join(options.output,'motion.mp4')});
    await renderer.renderStill({...common,frame:78,imageFormat:'jpeg',jpegQuality:100,output:path.join(options.output,'cover.jpg')});
    for (const frame of FRAMES) await renderer.renderStill({...common,frame,imageFormat:'png',output:path.join(options.output,`frame-${frame}.png`)});
    for (const name of ['motion.mp4','cover.jpg',...FRAMES.map(frame=>`frame-${frame}.png`)]) {
      const bytes = await fs.readFile(path.join(options.output,name));
      if (!bytes.length) fail(`Empty output: ${name}`);
      receipt.artifacts.push({path:name,bytes:bytes.length,sha256:sha(bytes)});
    }
    receipt.status = 'rendered_pending_visual_review';
    receipt.coverFrame = 78;
    receipt.completedAt = new Date().toISOString();
    receipt.workingSource = await fileTree(path.join(options.output,'src'));
    await fs.writeFile(receiptPath,JSON.stringify(receipt,null,2));
    console.log(JSON.stringify({status:receipt.status,outputDirectory:options.output,receipt:receiptPath,synthetic},null,2));
  } catch (error) {
    receipt.status = 'failed';
    receipt.failedAt = new Date().toISOString();
    receipt.error = {name:error.name,message:error.message};
    try {await fs.writeFile(receiptPath,JSON.stringify(receipt,null,2));} catch (receiptError) {console.error(`Receipt write failed: ${receiptError.message}`);}
    throw error;
  }
}

main().catch(error=>{console.error(JSON.stringify({status:'failed',message:error.message}));process.exitCode=1;});

``````

## scripts/run-video.ps1

SHA256：`0f1d28d01e9ef2bcbf462426e44266909fa1490b5ea58bc008fd30eb0205f08f`；对应节点：setup

``````text
[CmdletBinding()]
param(
  [ValidateSet('doctor','probe','core','transcribe','preview-voice','render','rough-prepare','rough-review','rough-suggest','rough-save','rough-export','fine-cut-plan','fine-cut-render','stage-status','stage-select','stage-save','stage-export','visual-task','visual-motion','visual-import-results','visual-asset-import','materials','talkcraft-export-words','talkcraft-import-edl','voice-doctor','voice-prepare','voice-synthesize','voice-enroll','voice-batch')]
  [string]$Command = 'doctor',
  [string]$Workspace = 'E:\开发项目\视频剪辑skill',
  [string]$ProjectDir,
  [string]$InputPath,
  [string]$OutputPath,
  [string]$Text,
  [string[]]$CoreArgs = @(),
  [string[]]$ToolArgs = @()
)

$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$runtimeRoot = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($Workspace)
$runtimeScript = Join-Path $runtimeRoot 'scripts\video.ps1'
$runtimeConfig = Join-Path $runtimeRoot 'config\video-workflow.json'
if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $runtimeScript -PathType Leaf) -or -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $runtimeConfig -PathType Leaf)) {
  throw "Configured video workspace is unavailable: $runtimeRoot. Set -Workspace to its current location; do not silently reinstall tools."
}
$configuration = Get-Content -LiteralPath $runtimeConfig -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
if ($configuration.kind -ne 'video-workspace-runtime-configuration' -or [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($configuration.workspaceRoot).TrimEnd('\') -ne $runtimeRoot.TrimEnd('\')) {
  throw 'Workspace configuration does not match this runtime root.'
}
$arguments = @{Command=$Command}
foreach ($argumentName in @('ProjectDir','InputPath','OutputPath','Text')) {
  if ($PSBoundParameters.ContainsKey($argumentName)) { $arguments[$argumentName]=$PSBoundParameters[$argumentName] }
}
if ($CoreArgs.Count -gt 0) { $arguments.CoreArgs=$CoreArgs }
if ($ToolArgs.Count -gt 0) { $arguments.ToolArgs=$ToolArgs }
& $runtimeScript @arguments
``````

## scripts/search-motion.mjs

SHA256：`9aa82670537e5b4513b96fb79f1414440bb1c7709838755bbbbbf346cddc076b`；对应节点：archive_reuse

``````text
#!/usr/bin/env node
/** Offline source discovery. Ranking is a retrieval aid, not an artistic decision. */
import fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const file = fileURLToPath(new URL('../assets/motion-library/case-index.json', import.meta.url));
const options = { limit: 6 };
const seenOptions = new Set();
for (let i = 2; i < process.argv.length; i += 2) {
  const flag = process.argv[i], value = process.argv[i + 1];
  if (!['--query', '--category', '--recipe', '--limit'].includes(flag) || value === undefined || value.startsWith('--')) {
    throw new Error('Usage: search-motion.mjs --query "口播 流程" [--recipe flow-steps] [--category explainer] [--limit 6]');
  }
  const key = flag.slice(2);
  if (seenOptions.has(key)) throw new Error(`Duplicate option: ${flag}`);
  seenOptions.add(key);
  options[key] = value;
}
options.limit = Number(options.limit);
if (!Number.isInteger(options.limit) || options.limit < 1 || options.limit > 30) throw new Error('limit must be 1..30');
if (options.category && !['motion', 'explainer', '3d', 'interactive'].includes(options.category)) throw new Error('Unknown category');
const recipes = ['title-reveal', 'photo-pan', 'flow-steps', 'comparison', 'checklist', 'metric-bars', 'quote-focus', 'lower-third'];
if (options.recipe && !recipes.includes(options.recipe)) throw new Error('Unknown recipe');
if (!options.query?.trim() && !options.category && !options.recipe) throw new Error('Provide query, category or recipe');
const catalog = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(file, 'utf8'));
const query = (options.query ?? '').toLocaleLowerCase().trim();
const words = query.split(/[\s,，、;；]+/).filter(Boolean);
const matches = catalog.cases.flatMap((entry) => {
  if (options.category && entry.category !== options.category) return [];
  if (options.recipe && !(entry.relatedRecipes ?? []).includes(options.recipe)) return [];
  const searchable = [entry.slug, entry.author, entry.category, ...(entry.remakeTechTags ?? []), entry.studyNote ?? '', ...(entry.searchTerms ?? []), ...(entry.relatedRecipes ?? [])].join(' ').toLocaleLowerCase();
  const matchedWords = words.filter((word) => searchable.includes(word));
  // A sentence query can also contain our compact Chinese topic aliases.
  const matchedAliases = (entry.searchTerms ?? []).filter((word) => query.includes(word.toLocaleLowerCase()));
  if (words.length && !matchedWords.length && !matchedAliases.length) return [];
  const score = matchedWords.length * 3 + matchedAliases.length * 2 + (entry.studyNote ? 1 : 0);
  return [{ ...entry, retrievalScore: score, matchedTerms: [...new Set([...matchedWords, ...matchedAliases])] }];
}).sort((a, b) => b.retrievalScore - a.retrievalScore || a.slug.localeCompare(b.slug));
console.log(JSON.stringify({
  kind: catalog.kind, query: options.query ?? null, sourceCommit: catalog.commit,
  snapshotCount: catalog.count, matchedCount: matches.length,
  caveat: catalog.caveat, results: matches.slice(0, options.limit),
}, null, 2));

``````

## scripts/tests/build-directed-motion.test.mjs

SHA256：`5a537d6574fbef244af17e701a3be22c25b569aff9ef61279464c13a83572a0a`；对应节点：setup

``````text
import test, { after } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { mkdir, mkdtemp, readFile, writeFile, stat } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { dirname, resolve, join } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';

const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const skillRoot = resolve(here, '../..');
const workspace = resolve(skillRoot, '../..');
const builder = join(skillRoot, 'scripts/build-directed-motion.mjs');
const checksParent = join(workspace, 'checks/2026-10-06/gap-closure/skill-supplement-v01');
await mkdir(checksParent, { recursive: true });
const checkRoot = await mkdtemp(join(checksParent, 'builder-tests-'));
const base = JSON.parse(await readFile(join(skillRoot, 'assets/directed-motion/life-buffer.example.json'), 'utf8'));
const runtime = JSON.parse((await readFile(join(workspace, 'config/video-workflow.json'), 'utf8')).replace(/^\uFEFF/u, ''));
const results = [];

async function runCase(name, input, expectation) {
  const inputPath = join(checkRoot, `${name}.json`), output = join(checkRoot, `${name}-engineering`);
  await writeFile(inputPath, JSON.stringify(input, null, 2), { flag: 'wx' });
  const execution = spawnSync(process.execPath, [builder, '--input', inputPath, '--output', output,
    '--gsap', runtime.render.motionGsapPath, '--font', runtime.render.motionFontPath ?? 'C:/Windows/Fonts/msyh.ttc'],
  { encoding: 'utf8', windowsHide: true });
  assert.equal(execution.status, expectation === true ? 0 : 1, execution.stderr);
  if (expectation !== true) {
    assert.match(execution.stderr, expectation);
    await assert.rejects(stat(output), /ENOENT/, 'invalid input must not produce an engineering directory');
  }
  results.push({ name, passed: true, actualExitCode: execution.status, stderr: execution.stderr.trim(), output });
  return output;
}

test('omitting the complete canvas cannot silently select 480P or 16 seconds', async () => {
  const input = structuredClone(base); delete input.canvas;
  await runCase('missing-canvas', input, /canvas must be an object/);
});
test('each required canvas dimension and timing field rejects omission', async () => {
  for (const field of ['width', 'height', 'fps', 'duration']) {
    const input = structuredClone(base); delete input.canvas[field];
    await runCase(`missing-${field}`, input, new RegExp(`canvas\\.${field} is required`));
  }
});
test('null, strings, nonpositive duration and fractional integer fields cannot become defaults', async () => {
  for (const [name, field, value, error] of [
    ['null-width', 'width', null, /canvas\.width must be/],
    ['string-fps', 'fps', '30', /canvas\.fps must be/],
    ['zero-duration', 'duration', 0, /canvas\.duration must be/],
    ['fractional-height', 'height', 480.5, /canvas width\/height\/fps must be integers/],
  ]) {
    const input = structuredClone(base); input.canvas[field] = value;
    await runCase(name, input, error);
  }
});
test('the existing explicitly specified insurance example still builds unchanged scene content', async () => {
  const output = await runCase('explicit-existing-example', structuredClone(base), true);
  const normalized = JSON.parse(await readFile(join(output, 'scene.json'), 'utf8'));
  assert.deepEqual(normalized.canvas, base.canvas);
  assert.equal(normalized.nodes.length, base.nodes.length);
  assert.equal(normalized.tracks.length, base.tracks.length);
  const html = await readFile(join(output, 'index.html'), 'utf8');
  assert.match(html, /data-width="854" data-height="480"/);
  assert.match(html, /data-duration="16"/);
});
test('a separately configured landscape composition keeps its actual dimensions and duration', async () => {
  const input = { schemaVersion: 1, compositionId: 'landscape-check', title: '参数验证',
    canvas: { width: 1920, height: 1080, fps: 24, duration: 4 },
    nodes: [{ id: 'title', kind: 'text', x: 960, y: 540, text: '明确画幅与时长' }] };
  const output = await runCase('explicit-landscape', input, true);
  assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(await readFile(join(output, 'scene.json'), 'utf8')).canvas, input.canvas);
  assert.match(await readFile(join(output, 'index.html'), 'utf8'), /data-duration="4" data-width="1920" data-height="1080"/);
});

after(async () => {
  await writeFile(join(checkRoot, 'validation.json'), JSON.stringify({ kind: 'explicit-canvas-builder-validation',
    casesPassed: results.length, cases: results, upstreamExecuted: false, renderingPerformed: false,
    note: 'Schema and actual output construction only; no new creative-quality claim or exported-video modification.' }, null, 2), { flag: 'wx' });
});

``````

## scripts/visible-text-policy.mjs

SHA256：`18295e15ba72e80dc6446e3d5b063ecfd3d16f38825ff091d8fd3c9adf125e90`；对应节点：qa

``````text
/** Policy for authored text rendered inside video frames, not metadata or paths. */
const PRODUCTION_LABELS = [
  { name: 'AI情境示意', pattern: /AI情境示意/iu },
  { name: 'AI示意', pattern: /AI示意/iu },
  { name: '初稿', pattern: /初稿/u },
  { name: '样片', pattern: /样片/u },
  { name: '未定稿', pattern: /未定稿/u },
  { name: '试片', pattern: /试片/u },
  { name: '工程小样', pattern: /工程小样/u },
  { name: '工程验证', pattern: /工程验证/u },
  { name: '工程演示文案', pattern: /工程演示文案/u },
];

/**
 * Validate one already parsed on-screen string and return it unchanged.
 * The label must identify the input field (for example shots[0].params.title).
 * NFKC and ignorable separators are used only for detection, so typography and
 * legitimate AI/technical subject matter are preserved in the returned value.
 */
export function assertVisibleText(value, label = 'visible text') {
  if (typeof value !== 'string') throw new Error(`${label}: on-screen text must be a string`);
  const comparable = value.normalize('NFKC').replace(/[\p{White_Space}\p{Cf}·•・|｜/\\_—–-]/gu, '');
  const forbidden = PRODUCTION_LABELS.find(({ pattern }) => pattern.test(comparable));
  if (forbidden) {
    throw new Error(`${label}: on-screen production label "${forbidden.name}" is not allowed; keep production status in delivery documents`);
  }
  return value;
}

``````

## SKILL.md

SHA256：`f5aff8c84056ca9a59e6a666b5f63e7ebd0d1f21f58b620a86d23f55fcbf6e9c`；对应节点：topic, setup, image, generation

``````text
---
name: jia-video
description: 王杰的视频生产入口，分为口播视频与无口播视频两条路线。口播以真人原片的校对、粗剪和精剪为基础；无口播从脚本或参考片制作分镜、素材与动画，可含旁白。支持参考片拆解交接、素材复用、本地本人配音和已有工程续作。
metadata:
  version: "3.1.1"
---

# Jia-video

支持两条同等完整的通路。将用户输入做成可审查的视频与可继续修改的工程，口播的粗剪、精剪不能被动效创作取代。根据内容先决定观看重点与可见变化，再选工具；标题、列表、图表有明确任务时正常使用。优先复用已配置工具、项目和真实获认可案例，仅要求某个阶段时停在对应范围。

本 Skill 的制作、剪辑和修改通路均不使用 ChatCut。复用现有本地 ASR、原创词表/EDL 桥、帧级精剪、内置 image_gen、HyperFrames/GSAP 与本地合成；不因为插件已安装或历史案例成功而调用它。此约束不涉及卸载其他任务使用的插件。

新增镜头优先检索并复用现成素材；生成只解决现有素材、图片和代码不能完成的需要。按阶段读对应参考，不一次加载全部方法、上游手册或研究日志。能力范围与模块职责见 [capabilities.md](references/capabilities.md)。

## 入口

| 用户给到的输入 | 通路 | 首先读取 |
|---|---|---|
| 真人口播、访谈原片，或准备录制真人讲解的视频 | 路线一：口播视频 | [talking-head.md](references/talking-head.md) |
| 无真人出镜口播，以动画、画面、录屏、实拍素材组织表达；可含旁白、音乐或无声 | 路线二：无口播视频 | [no-talking-head.md](references/no-talking-head.md) |
| 现有工程、初稿和修改意见 | 延续已有通路和断点 | 对应通路文件及工程记录 |

按成片主线选择路线，不按是否有视频文件选择。真人讲述是主线时走口播；产品片、代码动画、科普动画、MV、画面叙事走无口播。这里“无口播”指无真人出镜口播，不等于无旁白。已有连续录屏等素材需要剪辑时，无口播路线复用原片剪辑子流程。信息可以推断时自行判断并说明，仅在结果会明显改变范围时询问。

## 人工职责与风格落位

王杰自己负责选题、核心方向与发布决定。接到用户给定选题后再研究和制作；不默认调用选题 Skill 搜热点、评分或替换题目。用户另行要求时才协助比较选题。

AI执行、AI自检、王杰人工复核分别标注，不能互相代签。按 [AI与人工分工](references/ai-human-review.md) 交接，按 [风格与生产后端](references/style-routing.md) 选风格 Skill 与渲染后端。固定已认可风格规则，不固定每期画面；新风格或大改先做代表段供人工复核。

## 开始工作

1. 从工程记录和上下文提取用途、受众、目标时长、平台/画幅、素材路径、锁定台词、风格参考、交付要求和当前授权。探测真实素材，不把链接封面当成已看过视频。保留仍有效的脚本与表达。
2. 读取 [runtime.md](references/runtime.md)，运行本包 `scripts/run-video.ps1 -Command doctor`。它读取本机运行配置；不用重新安装浏览器、模型或修改全局环境。
3. 先读已有 brief、design、分镜、合同和版本。新项目使用 `E:\视频文档\YYYY-MM-DD_项目名`；同名工程续用或建明确新版本，保留原素材和旧输出。
4. 将制作判断写入简短 brief、脚本、分镜、设计和必要的事实记录：观看位置、重点、可见变化或稳定阅读区，以及为什么切镜。这些是制作材料，不是第二套审批合同。无真实 A-roll 的新代码创作可独立渲染，不把动画粗稿冒作原片强塞核心状态。

## 两条路线的关键顺序

- **路线一：口播视频。** 目标与台词/真人录制 → 原片登记与转写校对 → 语义粗剪并导出 → 精剪原声与切点并导出底片 → 补图、MG/B-roll → 整片审片、反馈、第二稿。原片未提供时先交付录制稿与拍摄清单，不伪造已剪辑。
- **路线二：无口播视频。** 参考拆解（如有）→ 核心表达与事实 → 脚本及声音/阅读节奏 → 分镜、主体与镜头空间 → 代表段及整片预演 → 资产制作、合成与审片 → 反馈、第二稿。代码、录屏、实拍、图片及生成片段按镜头需要组合。

口播精剪前可列补图需求，不先铺入镜头迫使台词迁就它们；粗剪、精剪各自保留视频、删改理由和时间映射。参考片需要反推风格、节奏和镜头时调用已启用的 `video-recreation`，接收拆解及创作交接；它不拥有成片字幕、最终音轨或第二套时间线。未启用时按当前工具完成拆解或说明缺口，不从隔离区执行候选。

用户已批准连续执行的阶段，不反复加审批门；已有核心的人工校对、真实付费执行和用户定稿门继续遵守。粗剪后的时间码需明确是原片还是输出片，不混用。

全片使用一条明确输出时间线，指定一个字幕 owner 和一个最终音轨 owner。组件提供文字/音效候选，由 owner 统一 cue 与混音，不重复烧录字幕或叠播本人原声。改词、剪切或换配音后重建受影响的映射。

## 导演判断先于模板

代码视频、解释插入和需要重做的动效，先读 [motion-direction.md](references/motion-direction.md)。按内容选择空间层次、主次延迟、动作预判与落定、惯性/受力、光影响应和镜头跟随。它们服务注意力与因果，不是每镜必须全开的特效菜单；没有声音时按阅读和事件建立节奏，不强加BGM。

需要动作解释时，让可见变化承载信息：对象变化、操作、关系或具体行为。文字可负责命名、条件、数字、标题和必要归纳；按语义决定构图，不强制每镜贯穿同一主体、运镜、碰撞、五层或持续运动。相机为观看目的服务，数据与稳定阅读段落可以静止。

先做最能暴露风格问题的代表段，实际看动作中间帧和连续播放。关键帧漂亮、所有元素有动画、技术QA通过，都不能证明整片有镜头感。按 [motion-quality.md](references/motion-quality.md) 单独审查动作、跨镜承接和整页重置。已有连续执行授权不因外部文章建议而增加固定等待确认门。

## 制作选择

取材读 [material-library.md](references/material-library.md)：`scripts/run-video.ps1 -Command materials` 统一执行本地检索、Commons/NASA无key查询、选定下载、Mixkit指定详情页下载、来源登记、SHA去重、完整解码和静音选段。Dareful及其他正常获取的授权视频可本地导入。复用缓存也验SHA/解码，失败不只看第三方退出码；大文件留在外部库。

需要在已保存视频中找具体画面和源时段，读 [material-semantic.md](references/material-semantic.md) 使用本地VideoSeek中文检索。缺素材时仍可能误匹配，分数不是正确率，不自动采用Top1。实际查看动作与上下文、核对许可、明确源入出点后重编码，检查真实时长，再经`visual-asset-import`导入既有第03步；不自动插片、不另起字幕或音轨。laohan只参考薄供应接口，MoneyPrinterTurbo只参考缓存经验，不整套装入它们的制作流程。

每个镜头承担明确的信息或情绪任务，说明为何采用用户素材、证据、代码、图片或生成视频。代码也能组织物体、空间、镜头与可重复的运动；精确数字、标签仍用可控排版。真实产品/操作用可核验素材。MiniMax默认只用于极少数有不可替代动作或叙事价值的镜头；一张图能表达同样内容时不付费生成视频。用户明确指定其他路线时服从当前范围和预算。

用 [shot-recipes.md](references/shot-recipes.md) 从镜头任务选择少量方法。查参考时读 [motion-library.md](references/motion-library.md)：保留Emil animate原指南、LiveCanvas折线源码、awesome-opus的475条离线索引。它们是方法或参考，不能把索引条数报成可执行组件数。高级定制使用本宿主已启用或明确暴露的能力，不从隔离区加载执行。

本机外部动效库与原创作品保存入口见 [motion-archive.md](references/motion-archive.md)。先按表达任务和实际参数协议查找，原库资源不代表本人已认可。用户认可且适合复用的原创动作保存预览、源码、参数、素材及 QA；技术验证只能暂存待审。大库、依赖和媒体保留在 E 盘外部库，Skill 仅保留检索与归档规则。

连续主体、分层空间和动作响应可使用新 [镜头执行层](references/directed-runtime.md) 的 `scripts/build-directed-motion.mjs`，或直接写符合后端接口的HTML/Remotion工程。执行层是确定性的2D场景图与运动近似，不是完整物理引擎或自动电影导演。先看其当前schema和实际支持范围，不虚构资产类型或相机能力。

原有 [8类组件](references/motion-components.md) 和 [基础合成器](references/composition.md) 保留用于局部字幕、标注、证据、明确需要的图表和媒体拼接。不要把它们的整页入场顺序当成完整视频默认导演方案。透明HTML层不等于普通MP4含alpha；复杂镜头不受基础构建器配方限制。

口播逐词审片使用 [rough-review.md](references/rough-review.md)：本地转写、保守剪辑建议、原片/粗剪预览与波形联动、恢复和校字，导出同源 MP4、SRT、原片→粗剪映射。继续在 [精剪与补图面板](references/review-stages.md) 调整帧级切点、导出精剪底片，再按精剪台词选择补图、制作本地代码动效或给 Codex 派发素材任务并合成。[fine-cut.md](references/fine-cut.md) 的独立 plan/render 入口仍保留。保存不代表用户确认；校字不删录音，局部裁剪已校对句子须重新核对保留片段字幕。可选口播词表、预剪 EDL 与 video-talkcraft 接入见 [talkcraft-adapter.md](references/talkcraft-adapter.md)：原创桥保留，上游仍是独立 staged、未启用候选；不复制整个上游、不执行自动升级，也不采用其第二套时间线和字幕默认。

时间趋势折线图可按 [data-charts.md](references/data-charts.md) 使用 `scripts/render-data-chart.mjs` 和已配置的 LiveCanvas 本地运行时。它读取带来源的数据，输出可编辑工程、MP4、结果帧及回执；当前原生规格是1080×1440、30fps、3秒，横竖版需要重新构图，不能只改输出尺寸。合成数据须显式标注，真实数据需核实来源。

## 声音、生成与运行边界

- 口播默认保留本人原声。新增旁白按 [personal-voice.md](references/personal-voice.md) 使用明确本人片段与准确原话建档、逐句本地合成及识别回读；本轮采用 DotTTS SOAR 的 audio.cpp 路线，生效引擎以 doctor 当前读回为准，MOSS 仅明确选择时兼容，不自动回退。模型验通不等于本人音色或试听已确认；Huihui 仅明确的离线试读，不能冒充本人或自动进入正式声音。
- 图片优先本宿主可用的内置生成工具，当前 Codex 为 image_gen。其他宿主先确认实际图像工具；没有时继续已有素材/代码镜头，若必需生成图则说明准确缺口。指定模型时不静默替换。
- MiniMax 接入状态以 [runtime.md](references/runtime.md) 指向的实时配置及有效回执为准，自动付费默认关闭；当前任务禁止使用时不调用。服务验通、预算授权、有效载荷和项目 claim 分开核对，不能用独立脚本绕过核心合同；缺口不阻碍独立本地工作。
- 修改台词/配音后更新字幕与时间线。ASR token 区间需要校对；不要把转写当成修改稿的强制对齐，或让字卡出现在对应台词之前。
- 所有视频画面禁止制作状态标签，包括AI示意、AI情境示意、初稿、样片、未定稿；状态与生成来源记录在交付文件。合同条件、数据单位等内容本体正常呈现。构建器的可见文本校验补充人工审查，无法替代对图片里已有文字的查看。

做有声动画或切换横竖版时读取 [voice-and-timing.md](references/voice-and-timing.md)，以实际音轨规划动作与字幕，重新排构图。已有项目的尺寸、字体和声音选择继续优先。

## 验收与交付

按 [acceptance.md](references/acceptance.md) 检查最终文件；代码动效另做 [动作与叙事审查](references/motion-quality.md)。最低交付为MP4、源码与运行说明、素材来源、技术QA、实际视觉/听审范围、创作问题及修改记录；口播另保留粗剪、精剪底片和时间映射。只完成某个阶段时如实说明，技术通过与用户确认分别记录。

可用 [media-inspection.md](references/media-inspection.md) 的 `scripts/inspect-video.mjs` 从最终 MP4 解码代表帧、切点邻帧和真实尾帧，记录哈希、全量解码与声音测量；之后实际查看图像和回听。它补充核心 `qa-media`，不代替核心状态、视觉判断或用户确认。

初稿交付后承接反馈，保留版本并给第二稿；仅用户明确确认后记录定稿。不要代签认可、付费批准或发布。沿用已有核心的版本、审片、最终确认与归档接口，交付后核验归档文件可读与来源完整。

``````
